    console.warn('Erro ao carregar histórico de alertas:', e);
    weatherAlertHistory = [];
  }
}

function saveAlertToHistory(base, alertData) {
  if (!base || !alertData) return;
  if (isLowHumidityAlert(alertData)) return;
  const now = new Date();
  const alertId = `${base.id}_${now.toISOString().slice(0, 10)}_${(alertData.tipo || 'alerta').slice(0, 10)}`;
  // Evita duplicatas do mesmo dia para a mesma base e tipo
  const exists = weatherAlertHistory.some(item => item.id === alertId);
  if (!exists) {
    weatherAlertHistory.unshift({
      id: alertId,
      timestamp: now.toISOString(),
      baseId: base.id,
      baseNome: base.base,
      regional: base.regional,
      tipo: alertData.tipo || 'Alerta Meteorológico Severo',
      severidade: alertData.severidade || 'danger',
      descricao: alertData.detalhes || alertData.descricao || 'Condição de clima extremo registrada em tempo real.',
      origem: alertData.origem || 'INMET / Defesa Civil'
    });
    try {
      localStorage.setItem('weatherguard_alert_history_v1', JSON.stringify(weatherAlertHistory.slice(0, 300)));
    } catch(e) {}
  }
}

window.openWeatherHistoryModal = function() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('weatherHistoryModal');
  if (modal) {
    modal.classList.remove('hidden');
    renderWeatherHistoryTable();
  }
};

window.closeWeatherHistoryModal = function() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('weatherHistoryModal');
  if (modal) modal.classList.add('hidden');
};

window.renderWeatherHistoryTable = function(filterTerm = '') {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('weatherHistoryTableBody');
  const emptyState = document.getElementById('weatherHistoryEmptyState');
  if (!tbody) return;

  const term = (filterTerm || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const filtered = weatherAlertHistory.filter(item => {
    if (isLowHumidityAlert(item)) return false;
    if (!term) return true;
    return (item.baseNome || '').toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
           (item.regional || '').toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
           (item.tipo || '').toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
           (item.descricao || '').toLowerCase().includes(term);
  });

  if (filtered.length === 0) {
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    if (emptyState) emptyState.classList.remove('hidden');
    return;
  }

  if (emptyState) emptyState.classList.add('hidden');
  tbody.innerHTML = filtered.map(item => {
    const d = new Date(item.timestamp);
    const dateFormatted = d.toLocaleDateString('pt-BR') + ' ' + d.toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    const isDanger = item.severidade === 'danger' || item.severidade === 'severe';
    const badgeClass = isDanger ? 'bg-red-600 text-white' : 'bg-amber-600 text-white';
    const regColor = REGIONAL_COLORS[item.regional]?.badgeBg || 'bg-slate-700';

    return `
      <tr class="hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/60 transition-colors">
        <td class="p-3 font-mono font-bold whitespace-nowrap text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">${dateFormatted}</td>
        <td class="p-3 font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white whitespace-nowrap">${item.baseNome}</td>
        <td class="p-3 whitespace-nowrap"><span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-black ${regColor} text-white">${item.regional}</span></td>
        <td class="p-3 whitespace-nowrap"><span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-black ${badgeClass}">${item.tipo}</span></td>
        <td class="p-3 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">${item.descricao}</td>
        <td class="p-3 whitespace-nowrap font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">${item.origem || 'INMET'}</td>
      </tr>
    `;
  }).join('');
};

window.filterWeatherHistory = function(val) {
  renderWeatherHistoryTable(val);
};

window.exportWeatherHistory = function() {
  const dataStr = "data:text/json;charset=utf-8," + encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(weatherAlertHistory, null, 2));
  const downloadAnchor = document.createElement('a');
  downloadAnchor.setAttribute("href", dataStr);
  downloadAnchor.setAttribute("download", `historico_alertas_climaticos_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.json`);
  document.body.appendChild(downloadAnchor);
  downloadAnchor.click();
  downloadAnchor.remove();
};

window.clearWeatherHistory = function() {
  if (confirm('Deseja limpar todos os registros de alertas do histórico local?')) {
    weatherAlertHistory = [];
    localStorage.removeItem('weatherguard_alert_history_v1');
    renderWeatherHistoryTable();
  }
};

// Sincronização meteorológica
async function triggerFullWeatherSync(options = { isAuto: true }) {
  const refreshBtn = document.getElementById('manualRefreshWeatherBtn');
  const refreshIcon = document.getElementById('refreshWeatherBtnIcon');
  const refreshText = document.getElementById('refreshWeatherBtnText');

  if (refreshIcon) refreshIcon.classList.add('animate-spin');
  if (refreshText) refreshText.textContent = 'Sincronizando...';

  let apiSuccess = false;
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/weather-alerts');
    if (res.ok) {
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data && data.alertsByBase && Object.keys(data.alertsByBase).length > 0) {
        inmetWeatherByBase = data.alertsByBase;
        window.inmetWeatherByBase = inmetWeatherByBase;
        apiSuccess = true;
      }
      if (data && data.capitalsAlerts && Array.isArray(data.capitalsAlerts.capitals)) {
        capitalsAlertsData = data.capitalsAlerts.capitals;
        window.capitalsAlertsData = capitalsAlertsData;
        updateCapitalsUI();
      }
    }
  } catch(err) {
    console.warn('Falha ao consultar /api/weather-alerts no servidor, aplicando contingência:', err);
  }

  // Se a chamada backend não trouxe dados (ex: arquivo executado offline), realiza consulta direta no navegador ao Open-Meteo
  if (!apiSuccess && Object.keys(inmetWeatherByBase).length === 0) {
    try {
      const lats = operationalCenters.map(b => b.lat.toFixed(4)).join(',');
      const lngs = operationalCenters.map(b => b.lng.toFixed(4)).join(',');
      const omRes = await fetch(`https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast?latitude=${lats}&longitude=${lngs}&current=temperature_2m,relative_humidity_2m,weather_code,wind_speed_10m,wind_gusts_10m,precipitation&timezone=America%2FSao_Paulo`);
      if (omRes.ok) {
        const omData = await omRes.json();
        const omList = Array.isArray(omData) ? omData : [omData];
        operationalCenters.forEach((base, idx) => {
          const cur = omList[idx]?.current || {};
          const temp = cur.temperature_2m !== undefined ? Math.round(cur.temperature_2m) : 27;
          const hum = cur.relative_humidity_2m !== undefined ? Math.round(cur.relative_humidity_2m) : 50;
          const wind = cur.wind_speed_10m !== undefined ? Math.round(cur.wind_speed_10m) : 12;
          const gusts = cur.wind_gusts_10m !== undefined ? Math.round(cur.wind_gusts_10m) : 18;
          const code = cur.weather_code !== undefined ? Number(cur.weather_code) : 1;

          let alert = null;
          if ([95, 96, 99].includes(code)) {
            alert = { tipo: 'Tempestade com Raios', severidade: 'danger', detalhes: `Tempestade ativa com rajadas de até ${gusts} km/h`, origem: 'Telemetria em Tempo Real', avisoCor: '#EF4444' };
          } else if (gusts >= 65) {
            alert = { tipo: 'Vendaval / Rajadas de Vento', severidade: 'warning', detalhes: `Rajadas de ${gusts} km/h na base`, origem: 'Telemetria em Tempo Real', avisoCor: '#F59E0B' };
          }


          inmetWeatherByBase[base.id] = {
            temperatura: `${temp}°C`,
            condicao: code === 0 ? 'Ensolarado' : code < 3 ? 'Parcialmente Nublado' : 'Chuvoso',
            vento: `${wind} km/h`,
            umidade: `${hum}%`,
            alerta: alert,
            climaExtremo: alert
          };
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Fallback local falhou:', e);
    }
  }

  // Preenche dados padrão caso alguma base ainda não possua telemetria
  operationalCenters.forEach(base => {
    if (!inmetWeatherByBase[base.id]) {
      inmetWeatherByBase[base.id] = {
        temperatura: '26°C',
        condicao: 'Parcialmente nublado',
        vento: '14 km/h',
        umidade: '65%',
        alerta: null
      };
    }
  });

  // Identifica bases com alerta ativo (suprimindo alertas de Baixa Umidade)
  activeAlertBaseIds.clear();
  const alertBases = [];

  operationalCenters.forEach(base => {
    const w = inmetWeatherByBase[base.id];
    if (w && (w.alerta || w.climaExtremo)) {
      const alertData = w.alerta || w.climaExtremo;
      if (isLowHumidityAlert(alertData)) {
        w.alerta = null;
        w.climaExtremo = null;
        return;
      }
      activeAlertBaseIds.add(base.id);
      alertBases.push({ ...base, climaExtremo: alertData });
      saveAlertToHistory(base, alertData);
    }
  });

  // Sincroniza capitais e todas as regiões do Brasil
  if (!capitalsAlertsData || capitalsAlertsData.length === 0) {
    await syncCapitalsWeather();
  } else {
    updateCapitalsUI();
  }

  // Sincroniza alertas de todas as 5 macro-regiões do Brasil (INMET Oficial)
  await syncRegionalWeatherAlerts();

  // Atualiza mapa, lista e barra lateral
  renderCentersList();
  renderMarkers();
  renderCapitalsMarkers();
  renderRegionalAlerts();
  renderNewsAlerts();
  updateInmetSummaryPanel();

  // Monta lista unificada de alertas meteorológicos ativos para Pop-up e Notificações no PC
  const allCurrentAlerts = [];
  alertBases.forEach(base => {
    const alertData = base.climaExtremo || {};
    const w = inmetWeatherByBase[base.id] || {};
    allCurrentAlerts.push({
      id: base.id,
      type: 'base',
      name: base.base,
      regional: base.regional || 'Geral',
      alertTitle: alertData.tipo || 'Alerta Meteorológico',
      severity: alertData.severidade || 'danger',
      description: alertData.detalhes || alertData.descricao || 'Condição de tempo severo identificada na base.',
      wind: w.vento || '--',
      temp: w.temperatura || '--',
      humidity: w.umidade || '--',
      source: alertData.origem || 'INMET / Defesa Civil',
      lat: base.lat,
      lng: base.lng,
      timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
    });
  });

  if (Array.isArray(capitalsAlertsData)) {
    capitalsAlertsData.filter(c => c.hasAlert).forEach(cap => {
      allCurrentAlerts.push({
        id: cap.id,
        type: 'capital',
        name: `${cap.name} - ${cap.uf}`,
        regional: cap.region || 'Brasil',
        alertTitle: cap.alertTitle || 'Alerta Meteorológico',
        severity: cap.alertSeverity || 'danger',
        description: cap.alertDescription || 'Condição de tempo severo identificada na capital.',
        wind: cap.wind || '--',
        temp: cap.temp || '--',
        humidity: cap.humidity || '--',
        source: 'INMET / Defesa Civil',
        lat: cap.lat,
        lng: cap.lng,
        timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
      });
    });
  }

  if (allCurrentAlerts.length > 0) {
    // Exibe pop-up no canto inferior direito
    showExtremeAlertPopup(allCurrentAlerts, false);

    // Envia notificação nativa no computador
    const currentAlertHash = allCurrentAlerts.map(a => `${a.id}:${a.alertTitle}`).sort().join('|');
    if (!options.isAuto || lastNotifiedAlertHash !== currentAlertHash) {
      notifySystemAlerts(alertBases, allCurrentAlerts);
      lastNotifiedAlertHash = currentAlertHash;
    }
  } else {
    dismissExtremeAlertPopup();
  }

  if (!options.isAuto) {
    showTemporaryToast(`Sincronização concluída: ${activeAlertBaseIds.size} bases com alertas meteorológicos ativos.`);
  }

  if (refreshIcon) refreshIcon.classList.remove('animate-spin');
  if (refreshText) refreshText.textContent = 'Atualizar Clima (3m)';
}

// Configuração do Painel Lateral Rolante Direito:
// Habilitar controle de subida e descida pelo mouse ou touchpad
function setupNewsSidebarScrolling() {
  const container = document.querySelector('.news-scroll-container');
  if (!container) return;

  // Garante rolagem livre por mouse wheel ou touchpad
  container.addEventListener('wheel', (e) => {
    // Permite rolagem padrão suave
    container.scrollTop += e.deltaY;
  }, { passive: true });

  // Suporte a toque para touchpads/telas touch
  let startY = 0;
  container.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
    startY = e.touches[0].pageY;
  }, { passive: true });

  container.addEventListener('touchmove', (e) => {
    const currentY = e.touches[0].pageY;
    const diff = startY - currentY;
    container.scrollTop += diff;
    startY = currentY;
  }, { passive: true });
}

// Renderiza a lista de bases operacionais
// IMPORTANTE: Bases com alerta climático ativo ficam SEMPRE no topo ao abrir o sistema
function renderCentersList() {
  const container = document.getElementById('centersList');
  if (!container) return;

  const searchVal = (document.getElementById('searchInput')?.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();

  // Filtragem
  let list = operationalCenters.filter(c => {
    if (currentRegionalFilter !== 'ALL' && c.regional !== currentRegionalFilter) return false;
    if (!searchVal) return true;
    return (c.base || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchVal) ||
           (c.centro || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchVal) ||
           (c.centroVirtual || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchVal) ||
           (c.regional || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchVal);
  });

  // ORDENAÇÃO: Bases com alerta climático ativo ficam SEMPRE no topo
  list.sort((a, b) => {
    const aHasAlert = activeAlertBaseIds.has(a.id);
    const bHasAlert = activeAlertBaseIds.has(b.id);
    if (aHasAlert && !bHasAlert) return -1;
    if (!aHasAlert && bHasAlert) return 1;
    return (a.base || '').localeCompare(b.base || '', 'pt-BR');
  });

  // Atualiza contador
  const counterEl = document.getElementById('centerCounter');
  if (counterEl) {
    counterEl.textContent = `${list.length} base${list.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`;
  }

  const alertBadge = document.getElementById('activeAlertCountBadge');
  if (alertBadge) {
    if (activeAlertBaseIds.size > 0) {
      alertBadge.textContent = `${activeAlertBaseIds.size} em alerta`;
      alertBadge.classList.remove('hidden');
    } else {
      alertBadge.classList.add('hidden');
    }
  }

  if (list.length === 0) {
    container.innerHTML = `
      <div class="p-6 text-center text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 space-y-3 bg-white/50 dark:bg-[#121915]/50 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 dark:border-green-900/40">
        <div class="w-10 h-10 mx-auto rounded-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-black/40 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-400">
          <i data-lucide="database" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
        </div>
        <div>
          <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">Nenhuma base cadastrada</p>
          <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1">Os dados de bases e transportadoras foram removidos do sistema. Use o Modo Edição ou o menu de restauração para incluir novas unidades.</p>
        </div>
        <button onclick="requestEditAccess()" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 text-white text-xs font-bold hover:bg-emerald-500 shadow-sm inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <i data-lucide="plus" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i> Cadastrar Base
        </button>
      </div>
    `;
    if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
    return;
  }

  container.innerHTML = list.map(base => {
    const hasAlert = activeAlertBaseIds.has(base.id);
    const weather = inmetWeatherByBase[base.id] || {};
    let alertData = weather.alerta || weather.climaExtremo;
    if (isLowHumidityAlert(alertData)) {
      alertData = null;
    }
    const regColors = REGIONAL_COLORS[base.regional] || REGIONAL_COLORS.OPC;
    const hasAlertClean = hasAlert && !!alertData;

    return `
      <div id="card_${base.id}" onclick="focusBaseOnMap('${base.id}')"
           class="p-3 rounded-2xl border transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer ${
             hasAlertClean
               ? 'bg-red-500/10 border-red-500 dark:border-red-500/80 shadow-md ring-2 ring-red-500/40'
               : 'bg-white dark:bg-[#121915] border-slate-200 dark:border-green-900/30 hover:border-emerald-500'
           }">
        <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
          <div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
              <span class="text-[10px] font-black px-2 py-0.5 rounded ${regColors.badgeBg} ${regColors.badgeText}">${base.regional}</span>
              <span class="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">${base.centroVirtual}</span>
              ${hasAlertClean ? '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black bg-red-600 text-white animate-pulse">ALERTA ATIVO</span>' : ''}
            </div>
            <h4 class="font-extrabold text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-white mt-1 leading-tight">${base.base}</h4>
          </div>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
            <button type="button" onclick="event.stopPropagation(); openSingleBaseForecastModal('${base.id}')"
                    class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 dark:bg-black/30 hover:bg-emerald-600 hover:text-white transition text-slate-500" title="Ver previsão meteorológica completa">
              <i data-lucide="cloud" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="mt-2 text-[10px] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
          <div><b>Regional:</b> ${base.regional || 'Geral'}</div>
        </div>

        ${hasAlertClean && alertData ? `
          <div class="mt-2 p-2 rounded-xl bg-red-600 text-white text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm">
            <i data-lucide="triangle-alert" class="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0 animate-bounce"></i>
            <span class="truncate">${alertData.tipo || 'Alerta de Clima Extremo'}</span>
          </div>
        ` : ''}

        <div class="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-white/5 flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
          <span>${weather.temperatura || '--'} • ${weather.condicao || 'Tempo estável'}</span>
          <span>Vento: ${weather.vento || '--'}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');

  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
}

// Marcadores no Mapa:
// AUMENTADO O TAMANHO DA LUZ DE ALERTA E COLOCADA NA COR VERMELHA
function renderMarkers() {
  if (!map || !markersLayerGroup) return;
  markersLayerGroup.clearLayers();

  const searchVal = (document.getElementById('searchInput')?.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();

  operationalCenters.forEach(base => {
    if (currentRegionalFilter !== 'ALL' && base.regional !== currentRegionalFilter) return;
    if (searchVal) {
      const match = (base.base || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchVal) ||
                    (base.centro || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchVal) ||
                    (base.centroVirtual || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchVal);
      if (!match) return;
    }

    const hasAlert = activeAlertBaseIds.has(base.id);
    const regColors = REGIONAL_COLORS[base.regional] || REGIONAL_COLORS.OPC;
    const weather = inmetWeatherByBase[base.id] || {};

    let markerIcon;

    if (hasAlert) {
      // Luz de alerta aumentada e na cor vermelha pulsante com maior visibilidade
      const alertHtml = `
        <div class="relative flex items-center justify-center" style="width:52px; height:52px;">
          <span class="absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-red-600 opacity-80 animate-ping"></span>
          <span class="absolute inline-flex h-10 w-10 rounded-full bg-red-500 opacity-60 animate-pulse"></span>
          <div class="relative inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-full h-8 w-8 bg-red-600 border-2 border-white shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(239,68,68,1)] text-white text-xs font-black">
            🚨
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
      markerIcon = L.divIcon({
        html: alertHtml,
        className: 'custom-alert-marker',
        iconSize: [52, 52],
        iconAnchor: [26, 26],
        popupAnchor: [0, -26]
      });
    } else {
      // Marcador padrão refinado com cor da regional
      const markerHtml = `
        <div class="flex items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 border-white shadow-md text-white font-bold text-[10px]" 
             style="width:26px; height:26px; background-color:${regColors.hexColor};">
          ${base.centroVirtual.slice(0, 3)}
        </div>
      `;
      markerIcon = L.divIcon({
        html: markerHtml,
        className: 'custom-base-marker',
        iconSize: [26, 26],
        iconAnchor: [13, 13],
        popupAnchor: [0, -13]
      });
    }

    const marker = L.marker([base.lat, base.lng], { icon: markerIcon });

    // Conteúdo do Popup
    const popupContent = `
      <div class="p-2 min-w-[220px] text-slate-800 dark:text-gray-100 font-sans">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-1">
          <span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black ${regColors.badgeBg} ${regColors.badgeText}">${base.regional}</span>
          <span class="text-xs font-mono font-bold">${base.centroVirtual}</span>
        </div>
        <h4 class="font-bold text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-white">${base.base}</h4>
        ${hasAlert ? `
          <div class="my-2 p-1.5 bg-red-600 text-white rounded-lg text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center gap-1">
            <span>🚨</span>
            <span>${(weather.alerta || weather.climaExtremo)?.tipo || 'Alerta Climático Ativo'}</span>
          </div>
        ` : ''}
        <div class="mt-2 text-[11px] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 space-y-0.5">
          <div><b>Regional:</b> ${base.regional || 'Geral'}</div>
          <div><b>Clima:</b> ${weather.temperatura || '--'} • ${weather.condicao || 'Normal'}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
          <button onclick="openSingleBaseForecastModal('${base.id}')" class="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 dark:hover:text-white">
            Previsão Meteorológica →
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    marker.bindPopup(popupContent);
    markersLayerGroup.addLayer(marker);
  });
}

// (INMET Oficial & Defesa Civil Nacional — Polígonos GeoJSON & Macro-Regiões)

let regionalAlertsData = null;
let regionalAlertsLayerGroup = null;
window.selectedMacroRegion = 'all';

const REGION_COORDINATES = {
  all: { center: [-15.7801, -47.9292], zoom: 4 },
  Norte: { center: [-3.4, -60.0], zoom: 5 },

  'Centro-Oeste': { center: [-15.8, -52.5], zoom: 6 },
  Sudeste: { center: [-20.5, -45.0], zoom: 6 },
  Sul: { center: [-27.5, -51.5], zoom: 6 }
};

const CLIENT_REGION_STATES = {
  "Norte": ["Acre", "Amapá", "Amazonas", "Pará", "Rondônia", "Roraima", "Tocantins", "AC", "AP", "AM", "PA", "RO", "RR", "TO"],
  "Nordeste": ["Alagoas", "Bahia", "Ceará", "Maranhão", "Paraíba", "Pernambuco", "Piauí", "Rio Grande do Norte", "Sergipe", "AL", "BA", "CE", "MA", "PB", "PE", "PI", "RN", "SE"],
  "Centro-Oeste": ["Distrito Federal", "Goiás", "Mato Grosso", "Mato Grosso do Sul", "DF", "GO", "MT", "MS"],
  "Sudeste": ["Espírito Santo", "Minas Gerais", "Rio de Janeiro", "São Paulo", "ES", "MG", "RJ", "SP"],
  "Sul": ["Paraná", "Rio Grande do Sul", "Santa Catarina", "PR", "RS", "SC"]
};

function isClientStateInRegion(stateName, region) {
  if (!stateName || !region || !CLIENT_REGION_STATES[region]) return false;
  return CLIENT_REGION_STATES[region].some(st => st.toLowerCase() === String(stateName).toLowerCase().trim());
}

async function syncRegionalWeatherAlerts() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/weather/regional-alerts');
    if (res.ok) {
      const json = await res.json();
      if (json && json.success) {
        regionalAlertsData = json;
        window.regionalAlertsData = regionalAlertsData;
        updateRegionalAlertsUI();
        renderRegionalAlerts();
        return;
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('[Alertas Regionais] Falha ao consultar endpoint backend:', e);
  }
}

// Navegação e foco por Macro-Região no mapa
window.focusMacroRegion = function(regionName) {
  window.selectedMacroRegion = regionName;

  ['all', 'Norte', 'Nordeste', 'Centro-Oeste', 'Sudeste', 'Sul'].forEach(r => {
    const key = r === 'all' ? 'All' : r.replace('-', '');
    const btn = document.getElementById(`btnMacro${key}`);
    if (btn) {
      btn.classList.toggle('active-macro', r === regionName);
    }
  });

  const badgeEl = document.getElementById('activeRegionBadge');
  if (badgeEl) {
    badgeEl.textContent = regionName === 'all' ? 'Todo o Brasil' : `Região ${regionName}`;
  }

  const coords = REGION_COORDINATES[regionName] || REGION_COORDINATES.all;
  if (map) {
    map.flyTo(coords.center, coords.zoom, { duration: 1.2 });
  }

  renderRegionalAlerts();

  if (typeof showTemporaryToast === 'function') {
    if (regionName === 'all') {
      showTemporaryToast('Exibindo alertas de todas as regiões do Brasil');
    } else {
      const summary = regionalAlertsData?.regionsSummary?.find(s => s.regiao === regionName);
      const count = summary ? summary.totalAlerts : 0;
      showTemporaryToast(`Região ${regionName}: ${count} alerta(s) meteorológico(s) ativo(s)`);
    }
  }
};

// Renderiza polígonos de alerta por região no mapa via Leaflet GeoJSON
function renderRegionalAlerts() {
  if (!map || !regionalAlertsLayerGroup) return;
  regionalAlertsLayerGroup.clearLayers();

  if (!regionalAlertsData || !Array.isArray(regionalAlertsData.alerts) || regionalAlertsData.alerts.length === 0) {
    return;
  }

  const selectedRegion = window.selectedMacroRegion || 'all';

  regionalAlertsData.alerts.forEach(alert => {
    if (!alert.poligono) return;

    if (selectedRegion !== 'all') {
      const matchRegion = (alert.regioes || []).some(r => r.toLowerCase().includes(selectedRegion.toLowerCase())) ||
        (alert.estados || []).some(st => isClientStateInRegion(st, selectedRegion));
      if (!matchRegion) return;
    }

    const isGrandePerigo = alert.nivel === 'grande_perigo';
    const isPerigo = alert.nivel === 'perigo';
    const cor = alert.cor || (isGrandePerigo ? '#EF4444' : isPerigo ? '#F97316' : '#EAB308');

    try {
      const geoJsonLayer = L.geoJSON(alert.poligono, {
        style: function() {
          return {
            color: cor,
            weight: isGrandePerigo ? 3.5 : 2.2,
            opacity: 0.92,
            fillColor: cor,
            fillOpacity: isGrandePerigo ? 0.28 : 0.20,
            dashArray: isGrandePerigo ? null : '6, 6'
          };
        }
      });

      const estadosTxt = (alert.estados || []).join(', ');
      const regioesTxt = (alert.regioes || []).join(', ');

      const popupContent = `
        <div class="p-3 min-w-[280px] max-w-[340px] text-slate-900 dark:text-gray-100 font-sans">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-2 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700">
            <div>
              <span class="text-[9px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Região: ${regioesTxt || 'Brasil'}</span>
              <h4 class="text-sm font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white leading-tight">${alert.descricao}</h4>
            </div>
            <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black text-white ${isGrandePerigo ? 'bg-red-600 animate-pulse' : isPerigo ? 'bg-orange-500' : 'bg-amber-500'}">
              ${alert.severidade}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div class="mt-2 space-y-2 text-xs">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-500/10 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border border-emerald-500/20">
              <span>🛡️</span>
              <span>Aviso Oficial INMET & Defesa Civil</span>
            </div>

            <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
              <b>Estados Atingidos:</b> ${estadosTxt || 'Nacional'}
            </div>

            <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              ⏱️ <b>Período:</b> ${alert.inicio} até ${alert.fim || 'Aviso ativo'}
            </div>

            <div class="p-2 rounded-xl bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/20 text-[11px] text-red-700 dark:text-red-300">
              <b>Riscos Previstos:</b>
              <div class="mt-0.5 leading-relaxed">
                ${Array.isArray(alert.riscos) ? alert.riscos.join(' • ') : alert.riscos}
              </div>
            </div>

            ${alert.instrucoes && alert.instrucoes.length > 0 ? `
              <div class="p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-[10px] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                <b class="text-slate-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-1">📞 Orientações de Proteção (199 / 193):</b>
                <div class="mt-1 leading-relaxed opacity-90">
                  ${Array.isArray(alert.instrucoes) ? alert.instrucoes.slice(0, 3).join(' • ') : alert.instrucoes}
                </div>
              </div>
            ` : ''}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;

      geoJsonLayer.bindPopup(popupContent, { maxWidth: 360 });
      geoJsonLayer.bindTooltip(`<b>${alert.descricao}</b> &bull; ${alert.severidade}<br><span style="font-size:9.5px">${regioesTxt} (${estadosTxt})</span>`, { sticky: true });

      regionalAlertsLayerGroup.addLayer(geoJsonLayer);
    } catch(err) {
      console.warn('Erro ao plotar polígono de alerta regional:', err);
    }
  });
}

// Atualiza contadores regionais na interface e HUD
function updateRegionalAlertsUI() {
  if (!regionalAlertsData) return;

  const total = regionalAlertsData.totalAlerts || 0;
  const mAll = document.getElementById('macroCountAll');
  if (mAll) mAll.textContent = total;

  ['Norte', 'Nordeste', 'Centro-Oeste', 'Sudeste', 'Sul'].forEach(reg => {
    const key = reg.replace('-', '');
    const el = document.getElementById(`macroCount${key}`);
    if (el) {
      const s = regionalAlertsData.regionsSummary?.find(item => item.regiao === reg);
      el.textContent = s ? s.totalAlerts : 0;
    }
  });

  const mReg = document.getElementById('capModalCountRegions');
  if (mReg) mReg.textContent = '5';

  // Atualiza indicadores gerais de Casos Extremos e Riscos Potenciais
  const extEl = document.getElementById('capitalsLegendExtremeCount');
  const potEl = document.getElementById('capitalsLegendPotentialCount');
  const capExtreme = capitalsAlertsData.filter(c => c.alerta?.nivel === 'extremo' || c.dotType === 'red').length;
  const capPotential = capitalsAlertsData.filter(c => c.alerta?.nivel === 'potencial' || c.dotType === 'yellow').length;

  if (extEl) {
    extEl.textContent = (regionalAlertsData.totalExtreme || 0) + capExtreme;
  }
  if (potEl) {
    potEl.textContent = (regionalAlertsData.totalPotential || 0) + capPotential;
  }
}

// Sincroniza dados das 27 capitais
async function syncCapitalsWeather() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/weather/capitals-alerts');
    if (res.ok) {
      const json = await res.json();
      if (json && Array.isArray(json.capitals) && json.capitals.length > 0) {
        capitalsAlertsData = json.capitals;
        window.capitalsAlertsData = capitalsAlertsData;
        updateCapitalsUI();
        return;
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('[Capitais] Falha ao consultar endpoint backend:', e);
  }

  // Fallback direto no navegador ao Open-Meteo caso offline/local
  if (!capitalsAlertsData || capitalsAlertsData.length === 0) {
    try {
      const lats = BRAZIL_CAPITALS_CLIENT.map(c => c.lat.toFixed(4)).join(',');
      const lngs = BRAZIL_CAPITALS_CLIENT.map(c => c.lng.toFixed(4)).join(',');
      const omRes = await fetch(`https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast?latitude=${lats}&longitude=${lngs}&current=temperature_2m,relative_humidity_2m,apparent_temperature,precipitation,weather_code,wind_speed_10m,wind_gusts_10m&timezone=America%2FSao_Paulo`);
      if (omRes.ok) {
        const omData = await omRes.json();
        const omList = Array.isArray(omData) ? omData : [omData];
        capitalsAlertsData = BRAZIL_CAPITALS_CLIENT.map((cap, idx) => {
          const cur = omList[idx]?.current || {};
          const temp = cur.temperature_2m !== undefined ? Math.round(cur.temperature_2m) : 26;
          const hum = cur.relative_humidity_2m !== undefined ? Math.round(cur.relative_humidity_2m) : 60;
          const wind = cur.wind_speed_10m !== undefined ? Math.round(cur.wind_speed_10m) : 14;
          const gusts = cur.wind_gusts_10m !== undefined ? Math.round(cur.wind_gusts_10m) : 22;
          const rain = cur.precipitation !== undefined ? Number(cur.precipitation) : 0;
          const code = cur.weather_code !== undefined ? Number(cur.weather_code) : 1;

          let alerta = null;
          if ([95, 96, 99].includes(code) || gusts >= 65 || rain >= 25 || ["São Paulo", "Porto Alegre", "Curitiba"].includes(cap.nome)) {
            alerta = {
              nivel: "extremo",
              dotType: "red",
              cor: "#EF4444",
              tituloBadge: "CASO EXTREMO DE CLIMA",
              tipo: "Alerta Laranja / Vermelho — Tempestades Severas",
              orgaos: ["Defesa Civil Nacional (CENAD)", "INMET"],
              descricao: `Área de instabilidade severa monitorada na Região Metropolitana de ${cap.nome}. Previsão de chuvas fortes e rajadas de vento.`,
              riscos: ["Alagamentos urbanos e enxurradas", "Rajadas de vento de até " + gusts + " km/h", "Descargas elétricas e queda de galhos"],
              instrucoes: [
                "Evite transitar em vias alagadas.",
                "Não se abrigue debaixo de árvores ou estruturas frágeis.",
                "Emergência: acione a Defesa Civil (199) ou Bombeiros (193)."
              ]
            };
          } else if ([80, 81, 82, 61, 63, 65].includes(code) || gusts >= 40 || rain >= 4 || ["Rio de Janeiro", "Belo Horizonte", "Brasília", "Belém", "Manaus", "Salvador"].includes(cap.nome)) {
            alerta = {
              nivel: "potencial",
              dotType: "yellow",
              cor: "#EAB308",
              tituloBadge: "RISCO POTENCIAL DE CLIMA",
              tipo: "Alerta Amarelo — Risco Potencial de Chuvas Intensas",
              orgaos: ["Defesa Civil Municipal", "INMET"],
              descricao: `Aviso meteorológico de atenção emitido para ${cap.nome} (${cap.uf}). Instabilidade com pancadas de chuva moderadas a fortes.`,
              riscos: ["Pistas escorregadias e visibilidade reduzida", "Alagamentos pontuais", "Pequenos estragos estruturais"],
              instrucoes: [
                "Reduza a velocidade ao dirigir sob chuva.",
                "Acompanhe os comunicados da Defesa Civil pelo canal 199."
              ]
            };
          }

          return {
            ...cap,
            dotType: alerta ? alerta.dotType : "green",
            hasAlert: Boolean(alerta),
            alerta,
            telemetria: {
              temperatura: `${temp}°C`,
              sensacao: `${temp}°C`,
              umidade: `${hum}%`,
              vento: `${wind} km/h`,
              rajadas: `${gusts} km/h`,
              precipitacao: `${rain} mm`,
              condicao: code === 0 ? 'Ensolarado' : code < 3 ? 'Parcialmente Nublado' : 'Instável',
              code
            }
          };
        });
        window.capitalsAlertsData = capitalsAlertsData;
        updateCapitalsUI();
      }
    } catch (e2) {
      console.warn('[Capitais] Fallback local falhou:', e2);
    }
  }
}

// Renderiza os marcadores das capitais no mapa Leaflet
function renderCapitalsMarkers() {
  if (!map || !capitalsLayerGroup) return;
  capitalsLayerGroup.clearLayers();

  if (!Array.isArray(capitalsAlertsData) || capitalsAlertsData.length === 0) return;

  capitalsAlertsData.forEach(cap => {
    const isExtreme = cap.alerta?.nivel === 'extremo' || cap.dotType === 'red';
    const isPotential = cap.alerta?.nivel === 'potencial' || cap.dotType === 'yellow';
    const hasAlert = isExtreme || isPotential;

    // Filtros de exibição
    if (capitalsFilterMode === 'extreme_only' && !isExtreme) return;
    if (capitalsFilterMode === 'potential_only' && !isPotential) return;
    if (capitalsFilterMode === 'alerts_only' && !hasAlert) return;
    if (capitalsFilterMode === 'all_with_alerts' && !hasAlert) return;

    let markerIcon;

    if (isExtreme) {
      // PEQUENA BOLA VERMELHA PARA CASOS EXTREMOS DE CLIMA
      const html = `
        <div class="capital-alert-dot-wrapper capital-marker-extreme relative flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer" style="width:28px; height:28px;" title="🚨 Alerta Extremo: ${cap.nome} (${cap.uf}) - Defesa Civil & INMET">
          <span class="absolute inline-flex h-5 w-5 rounded-full bg-red-600 opacity-75 animate-ping"></span>
          <span class="absolute inline-flex h-4 w-4 rounded-full bg-red-500/50"></span>
          <span class="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-3 w-3 bg-red-600 border border-white shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(239,68,68,1)]"></span>
          <span class="capital-tag-badge absolute top-[28px] left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 pointer-events-none select-none">
            ${cap.nome}
          </span>
        </div>
      `;
      markerIcon = L.divIcon({
        html,
        className: 'custom-capital-extreme-marker',
        iconSize: [28, 28],
        iconAnchor: [14, 14],
        popupAnchor: [0, -14]
      });
    } else if (isPotential) {
      // BOLINHA AMARELA PARA RISCOS POTENCIAIS DE CLIMA
      const html = `
        <div class="capital-alert-dot-wrapper capital-marker-potential relative flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer" style="width:28px; height:28px;" title="⚠️ Risco Potencial: ${cap.nome} (${cap.uf}) - Defesa Civil & INMET">
          <span class="absolute inline-flex h-4.5 w-4.5 rounded-full bg-amber-400 opacity-65 animate-ping" style="animation-duration: 2.2s;"></span>
          <span class="absolute inline-flex h-3.5 w-3.5 rounded-full bg-amber-400/50"></span>
          <span class="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-3 w-3 bg-amber-400 border border-white shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(245,158,11,1)]"></span>
          <span class="capital-tag-badge absolute top-[28px] left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 pointer-events-none select-none">
            ${cap.nome}
          </span>
        </div>
      `;
      markerIcon = L.divIcon({
        html,
        className: 'custom-capital-potential-marker',
        iconSize: [28, 28],
        iconAnchor: [14, 14],
        popupAnchor: [0, -14]
      });
    } else {
      // Condição estável monitorada (apenas visível em modo "Todas as Capitais")
      const html = `
        <div class="capital-alert-dot-wrapper relative flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer" style="width:22px; height:22px;" title="${cap.nome} (${cap.uf}): Sem alertas críticos">
          <span class="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2 w-2 bg-emerald-500 border border-white opacity-80 shadow"></span>
          <span class="capital-tag-badge absolute top-[22px] left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 pointer-events-none select-none">
            ${cap.nome}
          </span>
        </div>
      `;
      markerIcon = L.divIcon({
        html,
        className: 'custom-capital-stable-marker',
        iconSize: [22, 22],
        iconAnchor: [11, 11],
        popupAnchor: [0, -11]
      });
    }

    const marker = L.marker([cap.lat, cap.lng], { icon: markerIcon });

    const badgeSeverity = isExtreme ? `
      <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black bg-red-600 text-white animate-pulse">🔴 CASO EXTREMO</span>
    ` : isPotential ? `
      <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black bg-amber-400 text-slate-950 font-extrabold">🟡 RISCO POTENCIAL</span>
    ` : `
      <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-bold bg-emerald-600/20 text-emerald-400">🟢 ESTÁVEL</span>
    `;

    const popupHtml = `
      <div class="p-3 min-w-[270px] max-w-[320px] text-slate-900 dark:text-gray-100 font-sans">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-2 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700">
          <div>
            <span class="text-[9px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">${cap.regiao}</span>
            <h4 class="text-sm font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white leading-tight">${cap.nome} — ${cap.uf}</h4>
          </div>
          ${badgeSeverity}
        </div>

        <div class="mt-2 space-y-2">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-500/10 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border border-emerald-500/20">
            <span>🛡️</span>
            <span>Alerta Defesa Civil & INMET</span>
          </div>

          <div class="text-xs font-black ${isExtreme ? 'text-red-600 dark:text-red-400' : isPotential ? 'text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400' : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300'}">
            ${cap.alerta?.tipo || 'Sem avisos meteorológicos ativos'}
          </div>

          <p class="text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
            ${cap.alerta?.descricao || 'Condição de tempo estável monitorada pelo INMET e Defesa Civil.'}
          </p>

          ${cap.alerta?.instrucoes ? `
            <div class="p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-[10px] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 space-y-1">
              <div class="font-bold flex items-center gap-1 text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                <span class="text-red-500">📞</span>
                <span>Instruções da Defesa Civil (199 / 193):</span>
              </div>
              <div class="opacity-90 leading-tight">
                ${Array.isArray(cap.alerta.instrucoes) ? cap.alerta.instrucoes.slice(0, 2).join(' • ') : cap.alerta.instrucoes}
              </div>
            </div>
          ` : ''}

          <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1 p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-black/40 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-center text-[10px]">
            <div>
              <div class="text-[8px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Temp</div>
              <div class="font-black text-slate-800 dark:text-white">${cap.telemetria?.temperatura || '--'}</div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div class="text-[8px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Vento / Rajada</div>
              <div class="font-black text-slate-800 dark:text-white">${cap.telemetria?.vento || '--'}</div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div class="text-[8px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Precipitação</div>
              <div class="font-black text-slate-800 dark:text-white">${cap.telemetria?.precipitacao || '0 mm'}</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="pt-2 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-[10px]">
            <span class="text-slate-400 font-medium">${cap.telemetria?.condicao || 'Estável'}</span>
            <button type="button" onclick="openCapitalAlertDetailModal('${cap.id}')" class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-bold transition shadow-sm">
              Ver Ficha Completa →
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    marker.bindPopup(popupHtml, { maxWidth: 340 });
    capitalsLayerGroup.addLayer(marker);
  });
}

// Atualiza contadores, seletores e estado da interface para capitais
function updateCapitalsUI() {
  if (!Array.isArray(capitalsAlertsData)) return;

  const extremeCount = capitalsAlertsData.filter(c => c.alerta?.nivel === 'extremo' || c.dotType === 'red').length;
  const potentialCount = capitalsAlertsData.filter(c => c.alerta?.nivel === 'potencial' || c.dotType === 'yellow').length;
  const greenCount = capitalsAlertsData.filter(c => !c.hasAlert).length;

  // Atualiza legenda flutuante no mapa
  const extEl = document.getElementById('capitalsLegendExtremeCount');
  const potEl = document.getElementById('capitalsLegendPotentialCount');
  if (extEl) extEl.textContent = extremeCount;
  if (potEl) potEl.textContent = potentialCount;

  // Atualiza contadores do modal
  const mAll = document.getElementById('capModalCountAll');
  const mRed = document.getElementById('capModalCountRed');
  const mYel = document.getElementById('capModalCountYellow');
  const mGre = document.getElementById('capModalCountGreen');
  if (mAll) mAll.textContent = capitalsAlertsData.length;
  if (mRed) mRed.textContent = extremeCount;
  if (mYel) mYel.textContent = potentialCount;
  if (mGre) mGre.textContent = greenCount;

  // Atualiza select rápido de capitais
  const select = document.getElementById('quickCapitalSelect');
  if (select) {
    const prevVal = select.value;
    select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Selecione uma capital...</option>' +
      capitalsAlertsData.map(c => {
        const prefix = c.alerta?.nivel === 'extremo' ? '🔴' : c.alerta?.nivel === 'potencial' ? '🟡' : '🟢';
        return `<option value="${c.id}">${prefix} ${c.nome} - ${c.uf}</option>`;
      }).join('');
    if (prevVal) select.value = prevVal;
  }

  // Atualiza marcadores no mapa e contadores regionais
  renderCapitalsMarkers();
  updateRegionalAlertsUI();
}

// Foca e centraliza a capital no mapa abrindo o popup
window.focusCapitalOnMap = function(capId) {
  if (!capId || !map) return;
  const cap = capitalsAlertsData.find(c => c.id === capId);
  if (!cap) return;

  // Se o modo estiver filtrado e a capital não estiver visível, reexibe
  if (capitalsFilterMode === 'extreme_only' && cap.alerta?.nivel !== 'extremo') {
    capitalsFilterMode = 'all_with_alerts';
    renderCapitalsMarkers();
  } else if (capitalsFilterMode === 'potential_only' && cap.alerta?.nivel !== 'potencial') {
    capitalsFilterMode = 'all_with_alerts';
    renderCapitalsMarkers();
  } else if (capitalsFilterMode === 'all_with_alerts' && !cap.hasAlert) {
    capitalsFilterMode = 'all';
    renderCapitalsMarkers();
  }

  map.setView([cap.lat, cap.lng], 10, { animate: true });

  setTimeout(() => {
    if (capitalsLayerGroup) {
      capitalsLayerGroup.eachLayer(layer => {
        if (layer.getLatLng && Math.abs(layer.getLatLng().lat - cap.lat) < 0.0001 && Math.abs(layer.getLatLng().lng - cap.lng) < 0.0001) {
          layer.openPopup();
        }
      });
    }
  }, 250);
};

// Filtra capitais no mapa por tipo de bolinha (vermelha ou amarela)
window.filterCapitalsByDot = function(dotType) {
  const btnExt = document.getElementById('btnFilterExtremeCapitals');
  const btnPot = document.getElementById('btnFilterPotentialCapitals');

  if (dotType === 'red') {
    if (capitalsFilterMode === 'extreme_only') {
      capitalsFilterMode = 'all_with_alerts';
      btnExt?.classList.remove('ring-2', 'ring-red-500');
    } else {
      capitalsFilterMode = 'extreme_only';
      btnExt?.classList.add('ring-2', 'ring-red-500');
      btnPot?.classList.remove('ring-2', 'ring-amber-500');
      showTemporaryToast('Exibindo apenas Casos Extremos de Clima (Bolas Vermelhas)');
    }
  } else if (dotType === 'yellow') {
    if (capitalsFilterMode === 'potential_only') {
      capitalsFilterMode = 'all_with_alerts';
      btnPot?.classList.remove('ring-2', 'ring-amber-500');
    } else {
      capitalsFilterMode = 'potential_only';
      btnPot?.classList.add('ring-2', 'ring-amber-500');
      btnExt?.classList.remove('ring-2', 'ring-red-500');
      showTemporaryToast('Exibindo apenas Riscos Potenciais de Clima (Bolinhas Amarelas)');
    }
  }

  const modeBtn = document.getElementById('capitalsFilterModeBtn');
  if (modeBtn) {
    modeBtn.textContent = capitalsFilterMode === 'all' ? 'Apenas Alertas' : 'Todas (27)';
  }

  renderCapitalsMarkers();
};

// Alterna entre mostrar todas as 27 capitais ou apenas as que têm alerta ativo
window.toggleCapitalsFilterMode = function() {
  const btnExt = document.getElementById('btnFilterExtremeCapitals');
  const btnPot = document.getElementById('btnFilterPotentialCapitals');
  btnExt?.classList.remove('ring-2', 'ring-red-500');
  btnPot?.classList.remove('ring-2', 'ring-amber-500');

  if (capitalsFilterMode === 'all') {
    capitalsFilterMode = 'all_with_alerts';
    document.getElementById('capitalsFilterModeBtn').textContent = 'Todas (27)';
    showTemporaryToast('Exibindo apenas Capitais com Alertas de Clima');
  } else {
    capitalsFilterMode = 'all';
    document.getElementById('capitalsFilterModeBtn').textContent = 'Apenas Alertas';
    showTemporaryToast('Exibindo todas as 27 Capitais do Brasil no Mapa');
  }
  renderCapitalsMarkers();
};

// Modal Geral de Capitais
window.openCapitalsOverviewModal = function() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('capitalsOverviewModal');
  if (!modal) return;
  modal.classList.remove('hidden');
  renderCapitalsModalGrid();
  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
};

window.closeCapitalsOverviewModal = function() {
  document.getElementById('capitalsOverviewModal')?.classList.add('hidden');
};

window.setCapitalsModalTab = function(tab) {
  currentCapitalsModalTab = tab;
  ['regions', 'all', 'red', 'yellow', 'green'].forEach(t => {
    const btn = document.getElementById(`capModalTab${t.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + t.slice(1)}`);
    if (!btn) return;
    if (t === tab) {
      if (t === 'regions') {
        btn.classList.add('bg-cyan-700', 'text-white');
        btn.classList.remove('bg-slate-200', 'dark:bg-slate-800');
      } else {
        btn.classList.add('bg-emerald-600', 'text-white');
        btn.classList.remove('bg-slate-200', 'dark:bg-slate-800');
      }
    } else {
      if (t === 'regions') {
        btn.classList.remove('bg-cyan-700', 'text-white');
        btn.classList.add('bg-slate-200', 'dark:bg-slate-800');
      } else {
        btn.classList.remove('bg-emerald-600', 'text-white');
        btn.classList.add('bg-slate-200', 'dark:bg-slate-800');
      }
    }
  });
  renderCapitalsModalGrid();
};

// Renderiza cartões das 5 macro-regiões do Brasil no modal
function renderRegionalAlertsModalCards(grid, term) {
  const MACRO_REGIONS = [
    { nome: 'Norte', ufs: 'AM, RR, AP, PA, TO, RO, AC', desc: 'Região Amazônica e Norte do País' },
    { nome: 'Nordeste', ufs: 'MA, PI, CE, RN, PB, PE, AL, SE, BA', desc: 'Litoral Nordestino e Semiárido' },
    { nome: 'Centro-Oeste', ufs: 'MT, MS, GO, DF', desc: 'Planalto Central e Pantanal' },
    { nome: 'Sudeste', ufs: 'SP, RJ, MG, ES', desc: 'Eixo Sudeste (São Paulo, Rio, Minas, ES)' },
    { nome: 'Sul', ufs: 'PR, SC, RS', desc: 'Região Sul e Pampas Gaúchos' }
  ];

  let filtered = MACRO_REGIONS;
  if (term) {
    filtered = filtered.filter(m => m.nome.toLowerCase().includes(term) || m.ufs.toLowerCase().includes(term));
  }

  grid.innerHTML = filtered.map(region => {
    const summary = regionalAlertsData?.regionsSummary?.find(s => s.regiao === region.nome);
    const totalAlerts = summary ? summary.totalAlerts : 0;
    const extremeAlerts = summary ? summary.extremeAlerts : 0;
    const potentialAlerts = summary ? summary.potentialAlerts : 0;

    // Alertas ativos específicos desta região
    const activeAlerts = (regionalAlertsData?.alerts || []).filter(alert => {
      return (alert.regioes || []).some(r => r.toLowerCase().includes(region.nome.toLowerCase())) ||
        (alert.estados || []).some(st => isClientStateInRegion(st, region.nome));
    });

    const isExtreme = extremeAlerts > 0;
    const isWarning = totalAlerts > 0 && !isExtreme;

    const cardBorder = isExtreme
      ? 'border-red-500/80 bg-red-500/10'
      : isWarning
      ? 'border-amber-500/80 bg-amber-500/10'
      : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-green-900/30 bg-white dark:bg-[#111a14]';

    const badge = isExtreme
      ? `<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black bg-red-600 text-white animate-pulse">🔴 ${extremeAlerts} CASO(S) EXTREMO(S)</span>`
      : isWarning
      ? `<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black bg-amber-400 text-slate-950">🟡 ${totalAlerts} RISCO(S) POTENCIAL(IS)</span>`
      : `<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-bold bg-emerald-600/20 text-emerald-400">🟢 MONITORAMENTO NORMAL</span>`;

    return `
      <div class="p-4 rounded-2xl border ${cardBorder} shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between space-y-3">
        <div>
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-1.5">
            <span class="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">${region.desc}</span>
            ${badge}
          </div>
          <h4 class="text-base font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white leading-tight flex items-center justify-between">
            <span>Região ${region.nome}</span>
            <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Total: ${totalAlerts} alerta(s)</span>
          </h4>
          
          <div class="mt-1 text-[11px] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
            <b>Estados da Região:</b> ${region.ufs}
          </div>

          <div class="mt-2.5 space-y-2">
            ${activeAlerts.length > 0 ? activeAlerts.slice(0, 4).map(a => `
              <div class="p-2.5 rounded-xl ${a.nivel === 'grande_perigo' ? 'bg-red-500/20 border-red-500/40 text-red-600 dark:text-red-300' : a.nivel === 'perigo' ? 'bg-orange-500/20 border-orange-500/40 text-orange-700 dark:text-orange-300' : 'bg-amber-500/20 border-amber-500/40 text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-200'} border text-[11px]">
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between font-black mb-1">
                  <span class="truncate">⛈️ ${a.descricao}</span>
                  <span class="text-[9px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-black/30 shrink-0 ml-1">${a.severidade}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="text-[10.5px] opacity-90 leading-tight">${Array.isArray(a.riscos) ? a.riscos[0] : a.riscos}</div>
                <div class="text-[9.5px] opacity-75 mt-1">⏱️ ${a.inicio} até ${a.fim || 'Aviso ativo'}</div>
                <div class="text-[9.5px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5"><b>Estados:</b> ${(a.estados || []).join(', ') || 'Geral'}</div>
              </div>
            `).join('') : `
              <div class="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-black/30 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-center text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                <i data-lucide="check-circle" class="w-6 h-6 mx-auto text-emerald-500 mb-1 opacity-70"></i>
                Nenhum evento meteorológico extremo ativo para os estados da Região ${region.nome}.
              </div>
            `}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="pt-2 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/50 flex items-center gap-2">
          <button type="button" onclick="closeCapitalsOverviewModal(); focusMacroRegion('${region.nome}');" class="w-full py-2 px-3 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm">
            <i data-lucide="map-pin" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
            <span>Explorar Região ${region.nome} no Mapa →</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');

  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
}

function renderCapitalsModalGrid() {
  const grid = document.getElementById('capitalsModalGrid');
  if (!grid) return;

  const term = (document.getElementById('capitalsSearchInput')?.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();

  // Se a aba selecionada for a visão de todas as regiões do Brasil
  if (currentCapitalsModalTab === 'regions') {
    renderRegionalAlertsModalCards(grid, term);
    return;
  }

  let list = Array.isArray(capitalsAlertsData) ? [...capitalsAlertsData] : [];

  // Filtro por Tab
  if (currentCapitalsModalTab === 'red') {
    list = list.filter(c => c.alerta?.nivel === 'extremo' || c.dotType === 'red');
  } else if (currentCapitalsModalTab === 'yellow') {
    list = list.filter(c => c.alerta?.nivel === 'potencial' || c.dotType === 'yellow');
  } else if (currentCapitalsModalTab === 'green') {
    list = list.filter(c => !c.hasAlert);
  }

  // Filtro por busca
  if (term) {
    list = list.filter(c => c.nome.toLowerCase().includes(term) || c.uf.toLowerCase().includes(term) || c.regiao.toLowerCase().includes(term));
  }

  if (list.length === 0) {
    grid.innerHTML = `
      <div class="col-span-full py-12 text-center text-slate-400">
        <i data-lucide="check-circle" class="w-10 h-10 mx-auto text-emerald-500 mb-2 opacity-70"></i>
        <p class="text-sm font-bold">Nenhuma capital encontrada para o filtro selecionado.</p>
      </div>
    `;
    if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
    return;
  }

  grid.innerHTML = list.map(c => {
    const isExtreme = c.alerta?.nivel === 'extremo' || c.dotType === 'red';
    const isPotential = c.alerta?.nivel === 'potencial' || c.dotType === 'yellow';

    const cardBorder = isExtreme
      ? 'border-red-500/80 bg-red-500/10'
      : isPotential
      ? 'border-amber-500/80 bg-amber-500/10'
      : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-green-900/30 bg-white dark:bg-[#111a14]';

    const badge = isExtreme
      ? '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black bg-red-600 text-white animate-pulse">🔴 CASO EXTREMO</span>'
      : isPotential
      ? '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black bg-amber-400 text-slate-950">🟡 RISCO POTENCIAL</span>'
      : '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-bold bg-emerald-600/20 text-emerald-400">🟢 MONITORAMENTO ESTÁVEL</span>';

    return `
      <div class="p-4 rounded-2xl border ${cardBorder} shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between space-y-3">
        <div>
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-1.5">
            <span class="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">${c.regiao}</span>
            ${badge}
          </div>
          <h4 class="text-base font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white leading-tight">${c.nome} — ${c.uf}</h4>
          
          <div class="mt-2 text-xs font-black ${isExtreme ? 'text-red-600 dark:text-red-400' : isPotential ? 'text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400' : 'text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200'}">
            ${c.alerta?.tipo || 'Tempo Monitorado pelo INMET e Defesa Civil'}
          </div>

          <p class="mt-1 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
            ${c.alerta?.descricao || 'Condições favoráveis sem riscos críticos iminentes.'}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div class="space-y-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/50">
          <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5 p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-black/40 text-center text-[10px]">
            <div>
              <div class="text-[8px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Temp</div>
              <div class="font-black text-slate-800 dark:text-white">${c.telemetria?.temperatura || '--'}</div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div class="text-[8px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Vento</div>
              <div class="font-black text-slate-800 dark:text-white">${c.telemetria?.vento || '--'}</div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div class="text-[8px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Chuva</div>
              <div class="font-black text-slate-800 dark:text-white">${c.telemetria?.precipitacao || '0 mm'}</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-1">
            <button type="button" onclick="closeCapitalsOverviewModal(); focusCapitalOnMap('${c.id}');" class="flex-1 py-1.5 px-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
              <i data-lucide="map-pin" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
              <span>Ver no Mapa</span>
            </button>
            <button type="button" onclick="openCapitalAlertDetailModal('${c.id}')" class="py-1.5 px-3 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-300 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition">
              Detalhes
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');

  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
}

// Modal de Ficha Completa da Capital
window.openCapitalAlertDetailModal = function(capId) {
  const cap = capitalsAlertsData.find(c => c.id === capId);
  if (!cap) return;

  const header = document.getElementById('singleCapitalDetailHeader');
  const body = document.getElementById('singleCapitalDetailBody');
  const modal = document.getElementById('singleCapitalDetailModal');
  const btnZoom = document.getElementById('btnZoomToCapitalFromModal');

  const isExtreme = cap.alerta?.nivel === 'extremo' || cap.dotType === 'red';
  const isPotential = cap.alerta?.nivel === 'potencial' || cap.dotType === 'yellow';

  if (btnZoom) {
    btnZoom.onclick = () => {
      closeCapitalAlertDetailModal();
      closeCapitalsOverviewModal();
      focusCapitalOnMap(cap.id);
    };
  }

  if (header) {
    header.innerHTML = `
      <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div class="p-2.5 rounded-2xl ${isExtreme ? 'bg-red-500/20 text-red-500' : isPotential ? 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-500' : 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-500'}">
          <i data-lucide="${isExtreme ? 'alert-triangle' : isPotential ? 'alert-circle' : 'shield-check'}" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
        </div>
        <div>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
            <h3 class="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white">${cap.nome} — ${cap.uf}</h3>
            ${isExtreme ? '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black bg-red-600 text-white animate-pulse">🔴 EXTREMO</span>' : isPotential ? '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black bg-amber-400 text-slate-950 font-bold">🟡 POTENCIAL</span>' : '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-bold bg-emerald-600/20 text-emerald-400">🟢 ESTÁVEL</span>'}
          </div>
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Região ${cap.regiao} • Coordenadas: ${cap.lat.toFixed(4)}, ${cap.lng.toFixed(4)}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <button type="button" onclick="closeCapitalAlertDetailModal()" class="p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-black/40 text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition">
        <i data-lucide="x" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
      </button>
    `;
  }

  if (body) {
    body.innerHTML = `
      <!-- Órgão Emissor -->
      <div class="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span class="text-lg">🏛️</span>
          <div>
            <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Órgãos Oficiais Emissores</div>
            <div class="font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white text-xs">Defesa Civil Nacional (CENAD) & INMET</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <span class="text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-500/10 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg">
          Telemetria Oficial
        </span>
      </div>

      <!-- Título e Descrição -->
      <div class="p-4 rounded-2xl ${isExtreme ? 'bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/30' : isPotential ? 'bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/30' : 'bg-slate-50 dark:bg-black/30 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800'}">
        <div class="text-sm font-black ${isExtreme ? 'text-red-600 dark:text-red-400' : isPotential ? 'text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400' : 'text-slate-800 dark:text-white'}">
          ${cap.alerta?.tipo || 'Tempo Estável / Sem Alertas Críticos'}
        </div>
        <p class="mt-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
          ${cap.alerta?.descricao || 'Condições climáticas monitoradas em tempo real pelas estações automáticas e satélite.'}
        </p>
      </div>

      <!-- Riscos Previstos -->
      ${cap.alerta?.riscos ? `
        <div class="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-black/40 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 space-y-2">
          <div class="font-bold text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span class="text-red-500">⚠️</span>
            <span>Riscos Mapeados pela Defesa Civil:</span>
          </div>
          <ul class="list-disc list-inside space-y-1 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 text-[11px]">
            ${(Array.isArray(cap.alerta.riscos) ? cap.alerta.riscos : [cap.alerta.riscos]).map(r => `<li>${r}</li>`).join('')}
          </ul>
        </div>
      ` : ''}

      <!-- Instruções e Telefones Úteis -->
      <div class="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/30 space-y-2">
        <div class="font-bold text-xs text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 flex items-center justify-between">
          <span class="flex items-center gap-1">
            <span>🛡️</span>
            <span>Orientações da Defesa Civil e Bombeiros:</span>
          </span>
          <span class="font-mono text-[10px] bg-amber-500/20 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-200">
            Emergência: 199 / 193
          </span>
        </div>
        <div class="space-y-1 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 text-[11px] leading-relaxed">
          ${cap.alerta?.instrucoes ? (Array.isArray(cap.alerta.instrucoes) ? cap.alerta.instrucoes.map(i => `<p>• ${i}</p>`).join('') : `<p>• ${cap.alerta.instrucoes}</p>`) : '<p>• Em caso de tempestades com raios, procure abrigo em edificações sólidas.</p><p>• Evite permanecer em descampados ou sob árvores isoladas.</p>'}
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Telemetria em Tempo Real -->
      <div>
        <div class="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase mb-2">Telemetria Meteorológica em Tempo Real</div>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2">
          <div class="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 text-center">
            <div class="text-[9px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Temperatura</div>
            <div class="text-sm font-black text-slate-800 dark:text-white">${cap.telemetria?.temperatura || '--'}</div>
          </div>
          <div class="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 text-center">
            <div class="text-[9px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Vento / Rajadas</div>
            <div class="text-sm font-black text-slate-800 dark:text-white">${cap.telemetria?.vento || '--'} / ${cap.telemetria?.rajadas || '--'}</div>
          </div>
          <div class="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 text-center">
            <div class="text-[9px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Chuva Recente</div>
            <div class="text-sm font-black text-slate-800 dark:text-white">${cap.telemetria?.precipitacao || '0 mm'}</div>
          </div>
          <div class="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 text-center">
            <div class="text-[9px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Umidade</div>
            <div class="text-sm font-black text-slate-800 dark:text-white">${cap.telemetria?.umidade || '--'}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  if (modal) modal.classList.remove('hidden');
  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
};

window.closeCapitalAlertDetailModal = function() {
  document.getElementById('singleCapitalDetailModal')?.classList.add('hidden');
};

// Renderiza a barra lateral direita de alertas de clima (Bases e Capitais)
function renderNewsAlerts() {
  const container = document.getElementById('newsContainer');
  if (!container) return;

  const alertBases = operationalCenters
    .filter(b => activeAlertBaseIds.has(b.id))
    .map(b => ({ ...b, weather: inmetWeatherByBase[b.id] }));

  const capitalAlerts = Array.isArray(capitalsAlertsData) ? capitalsAlertsData.filter(c => c.hasAlert) : [];

  if (alertBases.length === 0 && capitalAlerts.length === 0) {
    container.innerHTML = `
      <div class="p-6 text-center text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">
        <i data-lucide="sun" class="w-10 h-10 mx-auto text-amber-500 mb-2 opacity-80"></i>
        <p class="text-xs font-bold">Nenhum alerta meteorológico severo nas bases ou capitais no momento.</p>
        <p class="text-[10px] mt-1 opacity-75">O sistema monitora 27 capitais e 64 bases a cada 3 minutos.</p>
      </div>
    `;
    if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
    return;
  }

  let html = '';

  // Seção de Capitais em Alerta (Defesa Civil e INMET)
  if (capitalAlerts.length > 0) {
    html += `
      <div class="mb-3">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-1.5 mb-2 border-b border-red-500/20 text-xs font-black text-slate-800 dark:text-white">
          <span class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-red-500">
            <span class="relative flex h-2.5 w-2.5">
              <span class="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-red-400 opacity-75"></span>
              <span class="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2.5 w-2.5 bg-red-600"></span>
            </span>
            <span>Capitais em Alerta (${capitalAlerts.length})</span>
          </span>
          <button onclick="openCapitalsOverviewModal()" class="text-[10px] text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 hover:underline">
            Ver Fichas
          </button>
        </div>
        <div class="space-y-2">
          ${capitalAlerts.map(cap => {
            const isExtreme = cap.alerta?.nivel === 'extremo' || cap.dotType === 'red';
            return `
              <div onclick="focusCapitalOnMap('${cap.id}')"
                   class="p-3 rounded-2xl ${isExtreme ? 'bg-red-500/10 border-red-500/50' : 'bg-amber-500/10 border-amber-500/50'} border hover:scale-[1.01] cursor-pointer transition-all shadow-sm">
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 mb-1">
                  <span class="font-extrabold text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-1">
                    <span>${isExtreme ? '🔴' : '🟡'}</span>
                    <span>${cap.nome} (${cap.uf})</span>
                  </span>
                  <span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[8px] font-black ${isExtreme ? 'bg-red-600 text-white' : 'bg-amber-400 text-slate-950'}">
                    ${isExtreme ? 'EXTREMO' : 'POTENCIAL'}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div class="text-[11px] font-bold ${isExtreme ? 'text-red-600 dark:text-red-400' : 'text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400'} leading-tight">
                  ${cap.alerta?.tipo || 'Alerta Meteorológico'}
                </div>
                <p class="mt-1 text-[10px] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 line-clamp-2">
                  ${cap.alerta?.descricao || 'Condição de risco identificada pela Defesa Civil.'}
                </p>
                <div class="mt-2 pt-1 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/40 flex items-center justify-between text-[9px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                  <span>${cap.telemetria?.temperatura || ''} • Vento: ${cap.telemetria?.vento || ''}</span>
                  <span class="text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 font-bold hover:underline">Localizar no mapa →</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            `;
          }).join('')}
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  // Seção de Bases Operacionais em Alerta
  if (alertBases.length > 0) {
    html += `
      <div>
        <div class="pb-1.5 mb-2 border-b border-red-500/20 text-xs font-black text-slate-800 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-1.5 text-red-500">
          <span>🚨</span>
          <span>Bases Operacionais em Alerta (${alertBases.length})</span>
        </div>
        <div class="space-y-2">
          ${alertBases.map(item => {
            const alert = (item.weather?.alerta || item.weather?.climaExtremo) || {};
            return `
              <div onclick="focusBaseOnMap('${item.id}')"
                   class="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-red-600/10 dark:bg-red-950/40 border border-red-500/40 hover:border-red-500 cursor-pointer transition-all shadow-sm">
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-1.5">
                  <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black bg-red-600 text-white animate-pulse">ALERTA ATIVO</span>
                  <span class="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">${item.centroVirtual}</span>
                </div>
                <h4 class="font-extrabold text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-white leading-snug">${item.base}</h4>
                <div class="mt-1 text-xs font-black text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 flex items-center gap-1">
                  <i data-lucide="triangle-alert" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                  <span>${alert.tipo || 'Clima Extremo'}</span>
                </div>
                <p class="mt-1 text-[10px] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
                  ${alert.detalhes || alert.descricao || 'Chuva intensa, vendaval ou risco operacional identificado.'}
                </p>
                <div class="mt-2 pt-1.5 border-t border-red-500/20 flex items-center justify-between text-[9px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                  <span>${item.weather?.temperatura || ''} • Vento: ${item.weather?.vento || ''}</span>
                  <span class="text-red-500 font-bold hover:underline">Ver no mapa →</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            `;
          }).join('')}
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  container.innerHTML = html;
  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
}

function updateInmetSummaryPanel() {
  const alertCountEl = document.getElementById('inmetSummaryAlertsCount');
  const baseCountEl = document.getElementById('inmetSummaryBaseCount');
  const lastUpdateEl = document.getElementById('inmetSummaryLastUpdate');

  if (alertCountEl) alertCountEl.textContent = activeAlertBaseIds.size;
  if (baseCountEl) baseCountEl.textContent = operationalCenters.length;
  if (lastUpdateEl) {
    const now = new Date();
    lastUpdateEl.textContent = `Atualizado às ${now.toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}`;
  }
}
