// Renderiza os marcadores de emergência no mapa Leaflet
function renderEmergencyMarkers() {
  if (!map || !emergencyLayerGroup) return;
  emergencyLayerGroup.clearLayers();

  EMERGENCY_POINTS_DATA.forEach(p => {
    // Filtros
    if (emergencyFilterType !== 'all' && p.tipo !== emergencyFilterType) return;
    if (emergencyFilterRegion !== 'all' && p.regiao !== emergencyFilterRegion) return;
    if (emergencySearchQuery) {
      const q = emergencySearchQuery.toLowerCase();
      const match = p.nome.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
                    p.sigla.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
                    p.cidade.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
                    p.uf.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
                    p.radioFrequencias.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      if (!match) return;
    }

    const isBombeiro = p.tipo === 'bombeiros';
    const beaconClass = isBombeiro ? 'bombeiros' : 'defesa-civil';
    const pingColor = isBombeiro ? 'bg-red-500' : 'bg-amber-400';
    const iconEmoji = isBombeiro ? '🚒' : '🛡️';

    const html = `
      <div class="emergency-map-marker" title="${p.sigla} (${p.uf}) - ${p.nome}">
        <div class="emergency-marker-beacon ${beaconClass}">
          <span class="emergency-marker-ping ${pingColor}"></span>
          <span class="relative z-10 select-none">${iconEmoji}</span>
        </div>
        <span class="emergency-marker-label">${p.sigla}</span>
      </div>
    `;

    const customIcon = L.divIcon({
      html,
      className: 'custom-emergency-marker',
      iconSize: [44, 46],
      iconAnchor: [22, 23],
      popupAnchor: [0, -22]
    });

    const marker = L.marker([p.lat, p.lng], { icon: customIcon });
    marker.emergencyPointId = p.id;

    const popupHtml = `
      <div class="p-1 min-w-[270px] max-w-[320px] text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2 pb-1.5 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-white/10">
          <span class="p-1.5 rounded-xl ${isBombeiro ? 'bg-red-600' : 'bg-amber-500'} text-white text-sm shadow">
            ${iconEmoji}
          </span>
          <div class="min-w-0">
            <div class="text-xs font-black leading-tight text-slate-900 dark:text-white">${p.sigla} - ${p.uf}</div>
            <div class="text-[9px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-semibold truncate">${p.cidade} • ${p.regiao}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-medium mb-2.5 leading-snug">
          📍 ${p.endereco}
        </div>

        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5 mb-2">
          <a href="tel:${p.telefoneEmergencia}" class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1 py-1.5 px-2 rounded-xl ${isBombeiro ? 'bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500' : 'bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500'} text-white text-[11px] font-black shadow transition">
            <i data-lucide="phone" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i> Ligar ${p.telefoneEmergencia}
          </a>
          <a href="https://wa.me/${p.whatsappClean}?text=Olá,%20solicito%20informações%20de%20emergência" target="_blank" class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1 py-1.5 px-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-[11px] font-black shadow transition">
            <i data-lucide="message-circle" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i> WhatsApp
          </a>
        </div>

        <div class="space-y-1.5 text-[9.5px] bg-slate-50 dark:bg-white/5 p-2 rounded-xl mb-2 border border-slate-200 dark:border-white/10">
          <div class="flex justify-between items-center text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
            <span class="font-bold">Linha Direta / Central:</span>
            <a href="tel:${p.telefonesDiretosClean}" class="text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 font-black hover:underline">${p.telefonesDiretos}</a>
          </div>
          <div class="text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 pt-1 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-white/10">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span class="font-bold flex items-center gap-1 text-slate-800 dark:text-white">
                <i data-lucide="radio" class="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-500"></i> Rádio VHF/UHF:
              </span>
              <button type="button" onclick="copyRadioFreq('${p.radioFrequencias.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', '${p.sigla}')" class="text-[8.5px] font-black text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 hover:underline">
                Copiar
              </button>
            </div>
            <div class="font-mono text-[9px] text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 mt-0.5 leading-snug font-semibold">${p.radioFrequencias}</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 pt-1 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-white/10 text-[9px]">
          <a href="${p.website}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400 font-bold hover:underline flex items-center gap-1">
            <i data-lucide="globe" class="w-3 h-3"></i> Página Oficial ↗
          </a>
          <button onclick="toggleEmergencySidebar(true); focusEmergencyPointOnMap('${p.id}');" class="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-800 dark:hover:text-white font-bold">
            Ver na Aba →
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    marker.bindPopup(popupHtml, { maxWidth: 320 });
    emergencyLayerGroup.addLayer(marker);
  });

  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
}

// Renderiza a lista de cards na aba lateral direita
function renderEmergencyHubList() {
  const container = document.getElementById('emergencyListContainer');
  const countBadge = document.getElementById('emergencyTotalCountBadge');
  if (!container) return;

  const filtered = EMERGENCY_POINTS_DATA.filter(p => {
    if (emergencyFilterType !== 'all' && p.tipo !== emergencyFilterType) return false;
    if (emergencyFilterRegion !== 'all' && p.regiao !== emergencyFilterRegion) return false;
    if (emergencySearchQuery) {
      const q = emergencySearchQuery.toLowerCase();
      return p.nome.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
             p.sigla.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
             p.cidade.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
             p.uf.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
             p.radioFrequencias.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
             p.telefonesDiretos.toLowerCase().includes(q);
    }
    return true;
  });

  if (countBadge) {
    countBadge.textContent = `${filtered.length} Unidades Cadastradas`;
  }

  if (filtered.length === 0) {
    container.innerHTML = `
      <div class="p-8 text-center text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">
        <i data-lucide="shield-alert" class="w-10 h-10 mx-auto text-red-500 mb-2 opacity-70"></i>
        <p class="text-xs font-bold">Nenhum ponto de socorro encontrado para os filtros selecionados.</p>
        <p class="text-[10px] mt-1">Tente buscar por outra cidade, estado ou limpe a busca.</p>
        <button onclick="clearEmergencySearch()" class="mt-3 px-3 py-1 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-xl shadow">Limpar Filtros</button>
      </div>
    `;
    if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
    return;
  }

  container.innerHTML = filtered.map(p => {
    const isBombeiro = p.tipo === 'bombeiros';
    const badgeBg = isBombeiro ? 'bg-red-600 text-white' : 'bg-amber-500 text-white';
    const badgeText = isBombeiro ? '🚒 Bombeiro Militar' : '🛡️ Defesa Civil';

    return `
      <div id="emergCard_${p.id}" class="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-[#121915] border border-slate-200 dark:border-red-500/20 shadow-md hover:border-red-500/50 transition-all flex flex-col gap-2">
        <!-- Topo: Tipo, UF e Botão Focar no Mapa -->
        <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
            <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider ${badgeBg}">
              ${badgeText}
            </span>
            <span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[9px] font-extrabold bg-slate-100 dark:bg-white/10 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
              ${p.uf} • ${p.regiao}
            </span>
          </div>
          <button type="button" onclick="focusEmergencyPointOnMap('${p.id}')" class="p-1 px-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 dark:bg-white/10 hover:bg-red-600 hover:text-white text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center gap-1 transition shadow-sm" title="Localizar quartel / sede no mapa">
            <i data-lucide="map-pin" class="w-3 h-3 text-red-500"></i>
            <span>Focar Mapa</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <!-- Nome e Endereço -->
        <div>
          <h4 class="text-xs font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white leading-snug">${p.nome}</h4>
          <p class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5 leading-tight flex items-center gap-1">
            <span>📍 ${p.endereco}</span>
          </p>
        </div>

        <!-- Contatos Rápidos: Emergência 193/199, Linha Direta e WhatsApp -->
        <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5 pt-1">
          <a href="tel:${p.telefoneEmergencia}" class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1 py-1.5 px-2 rounded-xl ${isBombeiro ? 'bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500' : 'bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500'} text-white text-[11px] font-black shadow transition" title="Ligar para socorro imediato">
            <i data-lucide="phone" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
            <span>${p.telefoneEmergencia}</span>
          </a>

          <a href="tel:${p.telefonesDiretosClean}" class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1 py-1.5 px-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-white/10 hover:bg-emerald-500/20 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 text-[9.5px] font-bold border border-slate-200 dark:border-white/10 transition" title="Ligar linha direta do COBOM / Defesa Civil">
            <i data-lucide="phone-forwarded" class="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-500"></i>
            <span class="truncate">${p.telefonesDiretos}</span>
          </a>

          <a href="https://wa.me/${p.whatsappClean}?text=Olá,%20solicito%20atendimento%20de%20emergência%20do%20Observatório%20do%20Clima" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1 py-1.5 px-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-[10px] font-bold shadow transition" title="Enviar mensagem via WhatsApp Oficial">
            <i data-lucide="message-circle" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
            <span>WhatsApp</span>
          </a>
        </div>

        <!-- Frequências de Rádio Operacional e Contingência -->
        <div class="mt-1 p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#0c120f] border border-slate-200 dark:border-emerald-500/20">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
            <span class="text-[9px] font-black uppercase text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 flex items-center gap-1">
              <i data-lucide="radio" class="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-500"></i>
              Frequências de Rádio Operacional
            </span>
            <button type="button" onclick="copyRadioFreq('${p.radioFrequencias.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', '${p.sigla}')" class="text-[8.5px] font-extrabold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 hover:underline flex items-center gap-0.5">
              <i data-lucide="copy" class="w-2.5 h-2.5"></i> Copiar
            </button>
          </div>
          <div class="text-[9.5px] font-mono text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 font-semibold leading-relaxed">
            ${p.radioFrequencias}
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Rodapé do Card: Website Oficial e Atendimento -->
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-1 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-white/5 text-[9.5px]">
          <span class="text-slate-400 font-medium">🕒 ${p.atendimento}</span>
          <a href="${p.website}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400 font-bold hover:underline flex items-center gap-1" title="Acessar portal oficial institucional">
            <i data-lucide="globe" class="w-3 h-3"></i>
            <span>Página da Internet ↗</span>
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');

  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
}

// Filtros da Aba de Socorro
window.setEmergencyFilterType = function(tipo) {
  emergencyFilterType = tipo;
  ['btnFilterEmergAll', 'btnFilterEmergBombeiros', 'btnFilterEmergDefesa'].forEach(id => {
    const btn = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!btn) return;
    btn.className = 'emergency-filter-chip px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-bold bg-slate-200 dark:bg-white/10 hover:bg-red-500/20 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 transition-all whitespace-nowrap';
  });

  const activeBtnId = tipo === 'all' ? 'btnFilterEmergAll' : (tipo === 'bombeiros' ? 'btnFilterEmergBombeiros' : 'btnFilterEmergDefesa');
  const activeBtn = document.getElementById(activeBtnId);
  if (activeBtn) {
    activeBtn.className = 'emergency-filter-chip px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-black bg-red-600 text-white shadow-sm transition-all whitespace-nowrap';
  }

  renderEmergencyHubList();
  renderEmergencyMarkers();
};

window.filterEmergencyHub = function() {
  const input = document.getElementById('emergencySearchInput');
  const regionSelect = document.getElementById('emergencyRegionSelect');
  const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clearEmergencySearchBtn');

  emergencySearchQuery = input ? input.value.trim() : '';
  emergencyFilterRegion = regionSelect ? regionSelect.value : 'all';

  if (clearBtn) {
    if (emergencySearchQuery) {
      clearBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
    } else {
      clearBtn.classList.add('hidden');
    }
  }

  renderEmergencyHubList();
  renderEmergencyMarkers();
};

window.clearEmergencySearch = function() {
  const input = document.getElementById('emergencySearchInput');
  const regionSelect = document.getElementById('emergencyRegionSelect');
  const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clearEmergencySearchBtn');
  if (input) input.value = '';
  if (regionSelect) regionSelect.value = 'all';
  if (clearBtn) clearBtn.classList.add('hidden');
  emergencySearchQuery = '';
  emergencyFilterRegion = 'all';
  renderEmergencyHubList();
  renderEmergencyMarkers();
};

// Alternância de visibilidade da aba lateral (Expandir / Recolher)
// Mostra no mapa os pontos de socorro apenas quando essa funcionalidade for acionada
window.toggleEmergencySidebar = function(forceOpen) {
  const sidebar = document.getElementById('emergencySidebar');
  if (!sidebar) return;

  const isHidden = sidebar.classList.contains('hidden');
  const shouldOpen = typeof forceOpen === 'boolean' ? forceOpen : isHidden;

  const quickBtn = document.getElementById('quickModeEmergencyBtn');
  const hudCb = document.getElementById('toggleEmergencyMapLayerCheckbox');
  const sideCb = document.getElementById('emergencyShowOnMapCheckbox');

  if (shouldOpen) {
    sidebar.classList.remove('hidden');
    sidebar.classList.add('flex');

    // Se outros painéis laterais estiverem abertos, fecha para evitar sobrecarga visual
    const news = document.getElementById('newsSidebar');
    if (news && !news.classList.contains('hidden')) {
      news.classList.add('hidden');
    }
    const routes = document.getElementById('wgRoutePlannerSidebar');
    if (routes && routes.classList.contains('is-open')) {
      routes.classList.remove('is-open');
      routes.classList.add('hidden');
    }

    // Mostra no mapa os pontos de socorro apenas quando essa funcionalidade for acionada
    if (map && emergencyLayerGroup) {
      if (!map.hasLayer(emergencyLayerGroup)) {
        emergencyLayerGroup.addTo(map);
      }
      renderEmergencyMarkers();
    }

    if (hudCb) hudCb.checked = true;
    if (sideCb) sideCb.checked = true;
    if (quickBtn) {
      quickBtn.classList.add('ring-2', 'ring-white', 'shadow-lg', 'bg-red-600');
      quickBtn.classList.remove('bg-red-800');
    }

    renderEmergencyHubList();
    if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
    setTimeout(() => { if (map) map.invalidateSize(); }, 200);
    showTemporaryToast('Aba de Socorro expandida • Pontos exibidos no mapa');
  } else {
    sidebar.classList.add('hidden');
    sidebar.classList.remove('flex');

    // Oculta do mapa os pontos de socorro quando a funcionalidade for recolhida
    if (map && emergencyLayerGroup && map.hasLayer(emergencyLayerGroup)) {
      map.removeLayer(emergencyLayerGroup);
    }

    if (hudCb) hudCb.checked = false;
    if (sideCb) sideCb.checked = false;
    if (quickBtn) {
      quickBtn.classList.remove('ring-2', 'ring-white', 'shadow-lg', 'bg-red-600');
      quickBtn.classList.add('bg-red-800');
    }

    setTimeout(() => { if (map) map.invalidateSize(); }, 200);
    showTemporaryToast('Aba de Socorro recolhida • Pontos ocultados do mapa');
  }
};

// Ligar/Desligar marcadores de emergência no mapa
window.toggleEmergencyMapMarkers = function(checked) {
  if (!map || !emergencyLayerGroup) return;

  const cb1 = document.getElementById('emergencyShowOnMapCheckbox');
  const cb2 = document.getElementById('toggleEmergencyMapLayerCheckbox');
  if (cb1) cb1.checked = checked;
  if (cb2) cb2.checked = checked;

  if (checked) {
    if (!map.hasLayer(emergencyLayerGroup)) {
      emergencyLayerGroup.addTo(map);
    }
    renderEmergencyMarkers();
    showTemporaryToast('Pontos de Socorro exibidos no mapa');
  } else {
    if (map.hasLayer(emergencyLayerGroup)) {
      map.removeLayer(emergencyLayerGroup);
    }
    showTemporaryToast('Pontos de Socorro ocultados do mapa');
  }
};

// Focar ponto específico no mapa
window.focusEmergencyPointOnMap = function(pointId) {
  const p = EMERGENCY_POINTS_DATA.find(x => x.id === pointId);
  if (!p || !map) return;

  // Garante que a camada está ligada
  if (!map.hasLayer(emergencyLayerGroup)) {
    emergencyLayerGroup.addTo(map);
    const cb1 = document.getElementById('emergencyShowOnMapCheckbox');
    const cb2 = document.getElementById('toggleEmergencyMapLayerCheckbox');
    if (cb1) cb1.checked = true;
    if (cb2) cb2.checked = true;
  }

  map.setView([p.lat, p.lng], 13, { animate: true });

  emergencyLayerGroup.eachLayer(layer => {
    if (layer.emergencyPointId === pointId) {
      layer.openPopup();
    }
  });

  // Rola o card na aba caso esteja visível
  const card = document.getElementById(`emergCard_${pointId}`);
  if (card) {
    card.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' });
    card.classList.add('ring-4', 'ring-red-500');
    setTimeout(() => card.classList.remove('ring-4', 'ring-red-500'), 2500);
  }
};

// Copiar frequência de rádio
window.copyRadioFreq = function(freq, sigla) {
  if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(freq);
  }
  showTemporaryToast(`Frequência de Rádio (${sigla}) copiada: ${freq}`);
};

// Modo Rápido: Socorro 193/199 (Alterna Expandir / Recolher)
window.wgQuickModeEmergency = function() {
  const routes = document.getElementById('wgRoutePlannerSidebar');
  if (routes) {
    routes.classList.remove('is-open');
    routes.classList.add('hidden');
  }
  toggleEmergencySidebar();
};

// Alternância da barra lateral direita:
// MODO PADRÃO: Recolhida por padrão
window.toggleNewsSidebar = function() {
  const sidebar = document.getElementById('newsSidebar');
  const toggleBtn = document.getElementById('newsSidebarToggleBtn');
  const toggleText = document.getElementById('newsSidebarToggleText');
  if (!sidebar) return;

  const isHidden = sidebar.classList.contains('hidden');
  if (isHidden) {
    sidebar.classList.remove('hidden');
    if (toggleText) toggleText.textContent = 'Fechar Alertas de Clima';
  } else {
    sidebar.classList.add('hidden');
    if (toggleText) toggleText.textContent = 'Abrir Alertas de Clima';
  }
};

// Alternância do menu cascata superior
window.toggleHeaderButtonsMenu = function() {
  const menu = document.getElementById('headerButtonsGroup');
  const btn = document.getElementById('toggleHeaderMenuBtn');
  if (!menu) return;
  const isHidden = menu.classList.contains('hidden');
  if (isHidden) {
    menu.classList.remove('hidden');
    menu.classList.add('flex');
    btn?.classList.add('bg-emerald-800');
  } else {
    menu.classList.add('hidden');
    menu.classList.remove('flex');
    btn?.classList.remove('bg-emerald-800');
  }
};

// Fechar menu superior ao clicar fora
document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  const menu = document.getElementById('headerButtonsGroup');
  const btn = document.getElementById('toggleHeaderMenuBtn');
  if (menu && !menu.classList.contains('hidden')) {
    if (!menu.contains(e.target) && !btn?.contains(e.target)) {
      menu.classList.add('hidden');
      menu.classList.remove('flex');
      btn?.classList.remove('bg-emerald-800');
    }
  }
});

// Base cartográfica Leaflet: Camadas Satélite e Topografia
let baseTileLayers = {};
let currentBaseLayerId = 'satelite';
let leafletLayerControl = null;

function updateCartographyUI(layerId) {
  if (layerId !== 'satelite' && layerId !== 'topografia') {
    layerId = 'satelite';
  }
  currentBaseLayerId = layerId;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem('wg_base_layer', layerId);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Não foi possível salvar preferência de camada:', err);
  }

  const btnSatelite = document.getElementById('btnLayerSatelite');
  const btnTopografia = document.getElementById('btnLayerTopografia');

  if (btnSatelite) btnSatelite.classList.toggle('active-layer', layerId === 'satelite');
  if (btnTopografia) btnTopografia.classList.toggle('active-layer', layerId === 'topografia');

  const nameEl = document.getElementById('currentCartographyName');
  if (nameEl) {
    if (layerId === 'topografia') {
      nameEl.textContent = 'Topografia & Relevo (OpenTopo)';
    } else {
      nameEl.textContent = 'Satélite Orbital (Alta Resolução)';
    }
  }
}
window.updateCartographyUI = updateCartographyUI;

window.setMapBaseLayer = function(layerId) {
  if (layerId !== 'satelite' && layerId !== 'topografia') {
    layerId = 'satelite';
  }
  if (!map || !baseTileLayers[layerId]) return;

  // Remove camadas base ativas
  Object.keys(baseTileLayers).forEach(id => {
    if (map.hasLayer(baseTileLayers[id])) {
      map.removeLayer(baseTileLayers[id]);
    }
  });

  // Adiciona a camada selecionada e posiciona atrás dos marcadores
  baseTileLayers[layerId].addTo(map);
  if (typeof baseTileLayers[layerId].bringToBack === 'function') {
    baseTileLayers[layerId].bringToBack();
  }

  updateCartographyUI(layerId);

  const layerLabels = {
    satelite: 'Camada Satélite Orbital',
    topografia: 'Camada Topografia e Relevo'
  };
  if (typeof showTemporaryToast === 'function') {
    showTemporaryToast(`Cartografia alterada: ${layerLabels[layerId] || layerId}`);
  }
};

// Inicialização do Mapa
function initMap() {
  const mapElement = document.getElementById('map');
  if (!mapElement) return;

  try {
    map = L.map('map', {
      center: [-15.7801, -47.9292],
      zoom: 4,
      zoomControl: true,
      minZoom: 3,
      maxZoom: 19
    });

    // 1. Camada Satélite (Esri World Imagery + Fronteiras e Topônimos Híbridos)
    const satelliteTiles = L.tileLayer('https://server.arcgisonline.com/ArcGIS/rest/services/World_Imagery/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}', {
      attribution: 'Tiles &copy; Esri &mdash; Source: Esri, Maxar, Earthstar Geographics, and GIS User Community',
      maxZoom: 19
    });
    const satelliteLabels = L.tileLayer('https://services.arcgisonline.com/ArcGIS/rest/services/Reference/World_Boundaries_and_Places/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}', {
      maxZoom: 19
    });
    const satelliteGroup = L.layerGroup([satelliteTiles, satelliteLabels]);

    // 2. Camada Topografia (OpenTopoMap com curvas de nível e relevo sombreado)
    const topoLayer = L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.opentopomap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', {
      attribution: 'Map data: &copy; <a href="https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright" target="_blank">OpenStreetMap</a> contributors, SRTM | Map style: &copy; <a href="https://opentopomap.org" target="_blank">OpenTopoMap</a>',
      maxZoom: 17
    });

    baseTileLayers = {
      satelite: satelliteGroup,
      topografia: topoLayer
    };

    // Aplica camada inicial (preferência salva ou Satélite)
    let savedLayer = 'satelite';
    try {
      const stored = localStorage.getItem('wg_base_layer');
      if (stored === 'topografia') savedLayer = 'topografia';
      else savedLayer = 'satelite';
    } catch(e) {}
    const activeLayer = baseTileLayers[savedLayer] || satelliteGroup;
    activeLayer.addTo(map);
    currentBaseLayerId = savedLayer;
    updateCartographyUI(currentBaseLayerId);

    markersLayerGroup = L.layerGroup().addTo(map);
    capitalsLayerGroup = L.layerGroup().addTo(map);
    regionalAlertsLayerGroup = L.layerGroup().addTo(map);
    emergencyLayerGroup = L.layerGroup(); // Exibido no mapa apenas quando acionado via Socorro 193/199

    // Controle nativo de camadas do Leaflet (L.control.layers)
    const baseMaps = {
      "🛰️ Satélite (Alta Resolução)": satelliteGroup,
      "⛰️ Topografia (Relevo / Curvas)": topoLayer
    };
    const overlayMaps = {
      "⛈️ Alertas por Região (Polígonos Brasil)": regionalAlertsLayerGroup,
      "🚨 Alertas das Capitais": capitalsLayerGroup,
      "🏢 Bases Operacionais": markersLayerGroup,
      "🚒 Socorro & Emergência (193/199)": emergencyLayerGroup
    };

    leafletLayerControl = L.control.layers(baseMaps, overlayMaps, {
      position: 'topright',
      collapsed: true
    }).addTo(map);

    // Sincroniza quando o usuário clica direto no controle nativo do Leaflet
    map.on('baselayerchange', function(e) {
      if (e.layer === satelliteGroup) updateCartographyUI('satelite');
      else if (e.layer === topoLayer) updateCartographyUI('topografia');
    });

    renderMarkers();
    renderCapitalsMarkers();
    renderRegionalAlerts();
    renderEmergencyMarkers();
    renderEmergencyHubList();
    if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Erro ao inicializar o mapa:', e);
    const fallback = document.getElementById('wgMapFallback');
    if (fallback) fallback.classList.remove('hidden');
  }
}

window.focusBaseOnMap = function(baseId) {
  const base = operationalCenters.find(b => b.id === baseId);
  if (!base || !map) return;
  map.setView([base.lat, base.lng], 12, { animate: true });

  // Abre popup se existir
  markersLayerGroup.eachLayer(layer => {
    if (layer.getLatLng && Math.abs(layer.getLatLng().lat - base.lat) < 0.0001 && Math.abs(layer.getLatLng().lng - base.lng) < 0.0001) {
      layer.openPopup();
    }
  });

  // Rola painel esquerdo até o card
  const card = document.getElementById(`card_${baseId}`);
  if (card) {
    card.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
    card.classList.add('ring-4', 'ring-emerald-500');
    setTimeout(() => card.classList.remove('ring-4', 'ring-emerald-500'), 2500);
  }
};

window.setRegional = function(reg) {
  currentRegionalFilter = reg;
  document.querySelectorAll('#regionalFiltersContainer .reg-btn').forEach(btn => {
    if (btn.getAttribute('data-reg') === reg) {
      btn.classList.add('ring-2', 'ring-white');
    } else {
      btn.classList.remove('ring-2', 'ring-white');
    }
  });
  renderCentersList();
  renderMarkers();
};

window.handleSearchInput = function(val) {
  renderCentersList();
  renderMarkers();
};

window.toggleSidebar = function() {};

window.toggleFullScreen = function() {
  if (!document.fullscreenElement) {
    document.documentElement.requestFullscreen().catch(() => {});
  } else {
    document.exitFullscreen().catch(() => {});
  }
};

window.wgGoHome = function() {
  // 1. Recolhe a coluna de Socorro & Emergência (e remove pontos do mapa)
  if (typeof toggleEmergencySidebar === 'function') {
    toggleEmergencySidebar(false);
  } else {
    const emergency = document.getElementById('emergencySidebar');
    if (emergency) {
      emergency.classList.add('hidden');
      emergency.classList.remove('flex');
    }
    if (map && emergencyLayerGroup && map.hasLayer(emergencyLayerGroup)) {
      map.removeLayer(emergencyLayerGroup);
    }
  }

  // 2. Recolhe a coluna lateral de Alertas de Clima Extremo
  const news = document.getElementById('newsSidebar');
  if (news && !news.classList.contains('hidden')) {
    news.classList.add('hidden');
    const toggleText = document.getElementById('newsSidebarToggleText');
    if (toggleText) toggleText.textContent = 'Abrir Alertas de Clima';
  }

  // 3. Recolhe o Planejador de Rotas Terrestres e limpa traçado
  if (typeof wgRouteClose === 'function') {
    wgRouteClose();
  }

  // 4. Recolhe menus suspensos e dropdowns
  const headerMenu = document.getElementById('headerButtonsGroup');
  if (headerMenu && !headerMenu.classList.contains('hidden')) {
    headerMenu.classList.add('hidden');
  }
  const configMenu = document.getElementById('configDropdownMenu');
  if (configMenu && !configMenu.classList.contains('hidden')) {
    configMenu.classList.add('hidden');
  }

  // 5. Fecha todos os modais abertos no sistema
  if (typeof closeRegionalsModal === 'function') closeRegionalsModal();
  if (typeof closeEditBasesModal === 'function') closeEditBasesModal();
  if (typeof closeCapitalsOverviewModal === 'function') closeCapitalsOverviewModal();
  if (typeof closeCapitalAlertDetailModal === 'function') closeCapitalAlertDetailModal();
  if (typeof closeInmetDetailModal === 'function') closeInmetDetailModal();
  if (typeof closeWeatherHistoryModal === 'function') closeWeatherHistoryModal();
  if (typeof dismissExtremeAlertPopup === 'function') dismissExtremeAlertPopup();

  // 6. Recolhe quadros flutuantes no mapa para deixar a visão limpa
  const capLegend = document.getElementById('capitalsAlertsLegend');
  if (capLegend && !capLegend.classList.contains('is-collapsed')) {
    capLegend.classList.add('is-collapsed');
  }

  // 7. Reseta filtros e buscas
  currentRegionalFilter = 'ALL';
  const searchInput = document.getElementById('searchInput');
  if (searchInput) searchInput.value = '';
  const emergSearch = document.getElementById('emergencySearchInput');
  if (emergSearch) emergSearch.value = '';
  document.querySelectorAll('#regionalFiltersContainer .reg-btn').forEach(btn => {
    if (btn.getAttribute('data-reg') === 'ALL') btn.classList.add('ring-2', 'ring-white');
    else btn.classList.remove('ring-2', 'ring-white');
  });

  // Reseta seletores e filtros de capitais
  capitalsDotFilter = 'all';
  capitalsFilterMode = 'all';
  const filterBtn = document.getElementById('capitalsFilterModeBtn');
  if (filterBtn) filterBtn.textContent = 'Todas (27)';
  const quickCapitalSelect = document.getElementById('quickCapitalSelect');
  if (quickCapitalSelect) quickCapitalSelect.value = '';

  // 8. Fecha popups e redefine visualização inicial do Brasil
  if (map) {
    map.closePopup();
    map.setView([-15.7801, -47.9292], 4, { animate: true });
    setTimeout(() => { if (map) map.invalidateSize(); }, 250);
  }

  // 9. Atualiza listagens e marcadores
  renderCentersList();
  renderMarkers();
  renderCapitalsMarkers();

  showTemporaryToast('Home: todas as funções recolhidas');
};

window.openRegionalsModal = function() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('regionalsModal');
  const grid = document.getElementById('regionalsGrid');
  if (!modal || !grid) return;

  grid.innerHTML = Object.entries(REGIONAL_MANAGERS).map(([reg, data]) => {
    const regColors = REGIONAL_COLORS[reg] || REGIONAL_COLORS.OPC;
    const basesCount = operationalCenters.filter(b => b.regional === reg).length;
    return `
      <div class="p-4 rounded-2xl border ${regColors.border} ${regColors.bg} space-y-2">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-black ${regColors.badgeBg} ${regColors.badgeText}">${reg}</span>
          <span class="text-xs font-bold">${basesCount} bases</span>
        </div>
        <h4 class="font-extrabold text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-white">${data.descricao || reg}</h4>
        <button onclick="setRegional('${reg}'); closeRegionalsModal();" class="mt-2 w-full py-1.5 bg-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">
          Filtrar bases de ${reg}
        </button>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');

  modal.classList.remove('hidden');
};

window.closeRegionalsModal = function() {
  document.getElementById('regionalsModal')?.classList.add('hidden');
};

window.openSingleBaseForecastModal = function(baseId) {
  const base = operationalCenters.find(b => b.id === baseId);
  const modal = document.getElementById('inmetDetailModal');
  const body = document.getElementById('inmetDetailBody');
  const title = document.getElementById('inmetDetailTitle');
  if (!modal || !body) return;

  const weather = inmetWeatherByBase[baseId] || {};
  if (title) title.textContent = `Previsão Meteorológica — ${base ? base.base : 'Base'}`;

  body.innerHTML = `
    <div class="p-4 space-y-3">
      <div class="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span class="text-sm font-bold">${base ? base.base : ''} (${base ? base.centroVirtual : ''})</span>
          <span class="text-2xl font-black text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400">${weather.temperatura || '--'}</span>
        </div>
        <p class="text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mt-1">${weather.condicao || 'Condição estável'}</p>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-3 text-xs">
          <div class="p-2 rounded bg-white dark:bg-slate-900"><b>Vento:</b> ${weather.vento || '--'}</div>
          <div class="p-2 rounded bg-white dark:bg-slate-900"><b>Umidade:</b> ${weather.umidade || '--'}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      ${(weather.alerta || weather.climaExtremo) ? `
        <div class="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-red-600 text-white font-bold">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
            <i data-lucide="triangle-alert" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
            <span class="text-sm uppercase">${(weather.alerta || weather.climaExtremo).tipo || 'Alerta Meteorológico'}</span>
          </div>
          <p class="text-xs font-normal text-white/90">${(weather.alerta || weather.climaExtremo).detalhes || (weather.alerta || weather.climaExtremo).descricao || ''}</p>
        </div>
      ` : '<p class="text-xs text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 font-bold">✓ Não há alertas meteorológicos severos vigentes para esta base.</p>'}
    </div>
  `;
  modal.classList.remove('hidden');
  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
};

window.closeInmetDetailModal = function() {
  document.getElementById('inmetDetailModal')?.classList.add('hidden');
};

window.openAllInmetForecastModal = function() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('inmetDetailModal');
  const body = document.getElementById('inmetDetailBody');
  const title = document.getElementById('inmetDetailTitle');
  if (!modal || !body) return;

  if (title) title.textContent = 'Monitoramento de Alertas e Clima Extremo';
  const alertBases = operationalCenters.filter(b => activeAlertBaseIds.has(b.id));

  body.innerHTML = `
    <div class="p-4 space-y-3">
      <div class="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-2">
        ${alertBases.length} base(s) com alerta ativo de um total de ${operationalCenters.length} bases monitoradas.
      </div>
      <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        ${operationalCenters.map(b => {
          const hasAlert = activeAlertBaseIds.has(b.id);
          const w = inmetWeatherByBase[b.id] || {};
          return `
            <div class="p-3 rounded-2xl border ${hasAlert ? 'border-red-500 bg-red-500/10' : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900'}">
              <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span class="font-bold text-xs">${b.base}</span>
                <span class="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400">${w.temperatura || '--'}</span>
              </div>
              <p class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1">${w.condicao || 'Normal'} • Vento: ${w.vento || '--'}</p>
              ${hasAlert ? `
                <div class="mt-2 p-1.5 rounded bg-red-600 text-white text-[9px] font-bold">
                  ${(w.alerta || w.climaExtremo)?.tipo || 'Alerta Climático'}
                </div>
              ` : ''}
            </div>
          `;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
  modal.classList.remove('hidden');
};

// Configuração das Notas Rápidas
window.toggleKdeNotesWidget = function() {
  const w = document.getElementById('kdeNotesWidget');
  if (!w) return;
  w.classList.toggle('hidden');
  renderWidgetNotes();
};

window.addNoteFromWidget = function() {
  const inp = document.getElementById('widgetNoteInput');
  if (!inp || !inp.value.trim()) return;
  systemNotes.unshift({ id: Date.now(), text: inp.value.trim(), date: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR') });
  inp.value = '';
  renderWidgetNotes();
};

window.deleteNote = function(id) {
  systemNotes = systemNotes.filter(n => n.id !== id);
  renderWidgetNotes();
};

function renderWidgetNotes() {
  const list = document.getElementById('widgetNotesList');
  if (!list) return;
  list.innerHTML = systemNotes.map(n => `
    <div class="p-2 rounded bg-slate-800 text-xs flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border border-slate-700">
      <div><span class="text-[10px] text-amber-400 font-mono">${n.date}</span> <span class="text-slate-200">${n.text}</span></div>
      <button onclick="deleteNote(${n.id})" class="text-slate-400 hover:text-red-400">✕</button>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

// Alternar recolhimento dos quadros flutuantes do mapa
window.toggleMapPanelCollapse = function(panelId) {
  const panel = document.getElementById(panelId);
  if (!panel) return;
  panel.classList.toggle('is-collapsed');
  const icon = panel.querySelector('.wg-panel-collapse-icon');
  if (icon) {
    if (panel.classList.contains('is-collapsed')) {
      icon.style.transform = 'rotate(180deg)';
    } else {
      icon.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)';
    }
  }
};

// Toast de feedback
window.showTemporaryToast = function(msg) {
  let toast = document.getElementById('systemGlobalToast');
  if (!toast) {
    toast = document.createElement('div');
    toast.id = 'systemGlobalToast';
    toast.className = 'fixed bottom-6 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-[10000000] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/95 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-2xl border border-emerald-500/40 pointer-events-none transition-all duration-300 opacity-0';
    document.body.appendChild(toast);
  }
  toast.textContent = msg;
  toast.classList.remove('opacity-0');
  toast.classList.add('opacity-100');
  clearTimeout(toast._timer);
  toast._timer = setTimeout(() => {
    toast.classList.remove('opacity-100');
    toast.classList.add('opacity-0');
  }, 3200);
};

// 1. Menu Superior em Cascata
window.toggleHeaderButtonsMenu = function(e) {
  if (e && e.stopPropagation) e.stopPropagation();
  const menu = document.getElementById('headerButtonsGroup');
  const btn = document.getElementById('toggleHeaderMenuBtn');
  if (!menu) return;
  const isHidden = menu.classList.contains('hidden');
  if (isHidden) {
    menu.classList.remove('hidden');
    menu.classList.add('flex');
    btn?.classList.add('bg-emerald-800');
    btn?.classList.remove('bg-emerald-700');
  } else {
    menu.classList.add('hidden');
    menu.classList.remove('flex');
    btn?.classList.remove('bg-emerald-800');
    btn?.classList.add('bg-emerald-700');
  }
  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
};

// Fecha menus ao clicar fora
document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  const menu = document.getElementById('headerButtonsGroup');
  const btn = document.getElementById('toggleHeaderMenuBtn');
  if (menu && !menu.classList.contains('hidden')) {
    if (!menu.contains(e.target) && !btn?.contains(e.target)) {
      menu.classList.add('hidden');
      menu.classList.remove('flex');
      btn?.classList.remove('bg-emerald-800');
      btn?.classList.add('bg-emerald-700');
    }
  }
  const configMenu = document.getElementById('configDropdownMenu');
  const configBtn = document.getElementById('configDropdownContainer');
  if (configMenu && !configMenu.classList.contains('hidden')) {
    if (!configMenu.contains(e.target) && !configBtn?.contains(e.target)) {
      configMenu.classList.add('hidden');
    }
  }
});

// 2. Modos Rápidos (Quick Mode)
window.wgQuickModeAlerts = function() {
  const news = document.getElementById('newsSidebar');
  const routes = document.getElementById('wgRoutePlannerSidebar');
  const emergency = document.getElementById('emergencySidebar');
  if (news) {
    news.classList.remove('hidden', 'sidebar-collapsed');
  }
  if (routes) {
    routes.classList.remove('is-open');
    routes.classList.add('hidden');
  }
  if (emergency && !emergency.classList.contains('hidden')) {
    toggleEmergencySidebar(false);
  }
  renderNewsAlerts();
  showTemporaryToast('Modo: Avisos de Clima Extremo ativado');
};

window.wgQuickModeRoutes = function() {
  const emergency = document.getElementById('emergencySidebar');
  if (emergency && !emergency.classList.contains('hidden')) {
    toggleEmergencySidebar(false);
  }
  wgRouteOpen();
  showTemporaryToast('Modo: Rotas ativado');
};

window.renderCentersListWithData = function() {
  renderCentersList();
};

window.toggleQuickModeButtons = function() {
  const nav = document.getElementById('quickModeButtons');
  const text = document.getElementById('toggleQuickModeButtonsText');
  const icon = document.getElementById('toggleQuickModeButtonsIcon');
  const btn = document.getElementById('toggleQuickModeButtonsBtn');
  if (!nav) return;
  const isHidden = nav.classList.contains('hidden') || nav.classList.contains('wg-quick-collapsed');
  if (isHidden) {
    nav.classList.remove('hidden', 'wg-quick-collapsed');
    if (text) text.textContent = 'Ocultar';
    if (icon) icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'chevron-left');
    btn?.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
  } else {
    nav.classList.add('hidden', 'wg-quick-collapsed');
    if (text) text.textContent = 'Reexibir';
    if (icon) icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'chevron-right');
    btn?.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
  }
  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
};

  menu.classList.toggle('hidden');
  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
};

const EDIT_MODE_PASSWORD = 'VibR@Exent777';
let editModeUnlocked = false;

window.requestEditAccess = function() {
  if (editModeUnlocked) {
    openEditBasesModal();
    return;
  }
  const password = window.prompt('Acesso restrito\nDigite a senha para liberar o Modo Edição:');
  if (password === EDIT_MODE_PASSWORD) {
    editModeUnlocked = true;
    openEditBasesModal();
    showTemporaryToast('Modo Edição liberado com sucesso.');
  } else if (password !== null) {
    alert('Senha incorreta. O Modo Edição permanece bloqueado.');
  }
};

window.toggleAlertPopups = function() {
  suspendPopups = !suspendPopups;
  const icon = document.getElementById('dropdownToggleAlertPopupsIcon');
  const text = document.getElementById('dropdownToggleAlertPopupsText');
  if (suspendPopups) {
    if (text) text.textContent = 'Reativar Pop-ups';
    if (icon) icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'bell-off');
    showTemporaryToast('Pop-ups de alerta suspensos.');
  } else {
    if (text) text.textContent = 'Suspender Pop-ups';
    if (icon) icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'bell');
    showTemporaryToast('Pop-ups de alerta reativados.');
  }
  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
};

window.downloadSystemData = function() {
  const data = {
    operationalCenters,
    regionalManagers: REGIONAL_MANAGERS,
    systemNotes,
    weatherAlertHistory,
    exportDate: new Date().toISOString()
  };
  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = `bases_operacionais_backup_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.json`;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  showTemporaryToast('Backup baixado com sucesso!');
};

window.downloadSystemCodeTxt = function() {
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = '/sistema_observatorio_clima_codigo_completo.txt';
  a.download = 'sistema_observatorio_clima_codigo_completo.txt';
  a.target = '_blank';
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
  showTemporaryToast('Download do arquivo TXT com o código completo iniciado!');
};

window.uploadSystemData = function(e) {
  const file = e?.target?.files?.[0];
  if (!file) return;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = function(evt) {
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(evt.target.result);
      if (Array.isArray(data.operationalCenters)) {
        operationalCenters = data.operationalCenters;
        window.operationalCenters = operationalCenters;
        localStorage.setItem('basesOperacionais_custom_v1', JSON.stringify(operationalCenters));
      }
      if (data.regionalManagers) {
        REGIONAL_MANAGERS = data.regionalManagers;
      }
      if (Array.isArray(data.weatherAlertHistory)) {
        weatherAlertHistory = data.weatherAlertHistory;
        localStorage.setItem('weatherguard_alert_history_v1', JSON.stringify(weatherAlertHistory));
      }
      renderCentersList();
      renderMarkers();
      showTemporaryToast('Dados restaurados com sucesso!');
    } catch(err) {
      alert('Erro ao importar arquivo JSON: ' + err.message);
    }
  };
  reader.readAsText(file);
};

window.testWeatherVoice = function() {
  if ('speechSynthesis' in window) {
    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    const utter = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance('Aviso vocal de alerta climático ativado e operacional na WeatherGuard.');
    utter.lang = 'pt-BR';
    utter.rate = 1.0;
    window.speechSynthesis.speak(utter);
    showTemporaryToast('Voz de alerta climático reproduzida com sucesso!');
  } else {
    showTemporaryToast('Síntese de voz não suportada pelo navegador.');
  }
};

window.toggleThemeMode = function() {
  const isDark = document.documentElement.classList.toggle('dark');
  localStorage.setItem('weatherguard_theme', isDark ? 'dark' : 'light');
  const icon = document.getElementById('themeIcon');
  const label = document.getElementById('themeLabel');
  if (isDark) {
    if (label) label.textContent = 'Modo Light';
    if (icon) icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'sun');
  } else {
    if (label) label.textContent = 'Modo Black';
    if (icon) icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'moon');
  }
  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
};

};

window.goToAlertBaseLocation = function(baseId) {
  const targetId = baseId || currentAlertBaseId || operationalCenters.find(b => b.climaExtremo?.ativo)?.id;
  if (!targetId) return;
  const base = operationalCenters.find(b => b.id === targetId);
  if (base && map) {
    map.setView([base.lat, base.lng], 13, { animate: true });
    closeExtremeAlertBox();
    openSingleBaseForecastModal(base.id);
  }
};

window.zoomToRegionalBases = function(reg) {
  closeRegionalsModal();
  setRegional(reg);
};

window.filterMeteorologicalForecasts = function(q) {
  const term = (q || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const body = document.getElementById('inmetDetailBody');
  if (!body) return;
  body.querySelectorAll('.inmet-detail-base').forEach(card => {
    card.style.display = card.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(term) ? '' : 'none';
  });
};

// 7. Planejador de Rotas Terrestres Open Source (OSRM / OpenStreetMap)
let routePoints = [];
let routePolyline = null;
let routeGlowPolyline = null;
let routeMarkers = [];

function initRoutePlanner() {
  if (routePoints.length === 0 && operationalCenters.length >= 2) {
    routePoints = [
      { id: 1, name: operationalCenters[0].base, lat: operationalCenters[0].lat, lng: operationalCenters[0].lng },
      { id: 2, name: operationalCenters[1].base, lat: operationalCenters[1].lat, lng: operationalCenters[1].lng }
    ];
  } else if (routePoints.length === 0 && typeof BRAZIL_CAPITALS_CLIENT !== 'undefined' && BRAZIL_CAPITALS_CLIENT.length >= 2) {
    routePoints = [
      { id: 1, name: 'Brasília (DF)', lat: -15.7975, lng: -47.8919 },
      { id: 2, name: 'São Paulo (SP)', lat: -23.5505, lng: -46.6333 }
    ];
  } else if (routePoints.length === 0) {
    routePoints = [
      { id: 1, name: 'Ponto de Partida', lat: -15.7988, lng: -47.9757 },
      { id: 2, name: 'Ponto de Destino', lat: -23.5505, lng: -46.6333 }
    ];
  }
  renderRoutePoints();
}

window.wgRouteOpen = function() {
  const r = document.getElementById('wgRoutePlannerSidebar');
  if (r) {
    r.classList.add('is-open');
    r.classList.remove('hidden');
  }
  const news = document.getElementById('newsSidebar');
  if (news) news.classList.add('hidden');

  if (routePoints.length === 0) {
    initRoutePlanner();
  }
};

window.wgRouteClose = function() {
  const r = document.getElementById('wgRoutePlannerSidebar');
  if (r) {
    r.classList.remove('is-open');
    r.classList.add('hidden');
  }
  if (routePolyline && map) {
    map.removeLayer(routePolyline);
    routePolyline = null;
  }
  if (routeGlowPolyline && map) {
    map.removeLayer(routeGlowPolyline);
    routeGlowPolyline = null;
  }
  routeMarkers.forEach(m => map && map.removeLayer(m));
  routeMarkers = [];
};

function renderRoutePoints() {
  const container = document.getElementById('wgRpPoints');
  const sel = document.getElementById('wgRpLastPoint');
  if (!container) return;
  container.innerHTML = routePoints.map((p, idx) => `
    <div class="wg-rp-point flex items-center gap-2 mb-2 p-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-white/5 border border-slate-200 dark:border-white/10">
      <span class="wg-rp-badge">${idx + 1}</span>
      <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
        <input class="wg-rp-input" value="${p.name}" onchange="updateRoutePointName(${idx}, this.value)" placeholder="Nome ou referência">
      </div>
      <button onclick="removeRoutePoint(${idx})" class="p-1 text-red-500 hover:text-red-400 font-bold" title="Remover ponto">✕</button>
    </div>
  `).join('');

  if (sel) {
    sel.innerHTML = routePoints.map((p, idx) => `<option value="${idx}" ${idx === routePoints.length - 1 ? 'selected' : ''}>Ponto ${idx+1}: ${p.name}</option>`).join('');
  }
}

window.updateRoutePointName = function(idx, val) {
  if (routePoints[idx]) routePoints[idx].name = val;
};

window.removeRoutePoint = function(idx) {
  if (routePoints.length <= 2) {
    showTemporaryToast('A rota necessita de no mínimo 2 pontos.');
    return;
  }
  routePoints.splice(idx, 1);
  renderRoutePoints();
};

window.wgRouteAddPoint = function() {
  let defaultPt = null;
  if (operationalCenters && operationalCenters.length > 0) {
    defaultPt = operationalCenters[routePoints.length % operationalCenters.length];
  } else if (typeof BRAZIL_CAPITALS_CLIENT !== 'undefined' && BRAZIL_CAPITALS_CLIENT.length > 0) {
    defaultPt = BRAZIL_CAPITALS_CLIENT[routePoints.length % BRAZIL_CAPITALS_CLIENT.length];
  }
  routePoints.push({
    id: Date.now(),
    name: defaultPt ? (defaultPt.base || defaultPt.nome) : `Ponto ${routePoints.length + 1}`,
    lat: defaultPt ? defaultPt.lat : -15.7988,
    lng: defaultPt ? defaultPt.lng : -47.9757
  });
  renderRoutePoints();
};

window.wgRouteClear = function() {
  routePoints = [];
  renderRoutePoints();
  const legs = document.getElementById('wgRpLegs');
  const sum = document.getElementById('wgRpSummary');
  if (legs) legs.innerHTML = '';
  if (sum) sum.innerHTML = '';
  if (routePolyline && map) {
    map.removeLayer(routePolyline);
    routePolyline = null;
  }
  if (routeGlowPolyline && map) {
    map.removeLayer(routeGlowPolyline);
    routeGlowPolyline = null;
  }
  routeMarkers.forEach(m => map && map.removeLayer(m));
  routeMarkers = [];
};

function calculateHaversineKm(lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2) {
  const R = 6371;
  const dLat = (lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI / 180;
  const dLon = (lon2 - lon1) * Math.PI / 180;
  const a = Math.sin(dLat/2) * Math.sin(dLat/2) +
            Math.cos(lat1 * Math.PI / 180) * Math.cos(lat2 * Math.PI / 180) *
            Math.sin(dLon/2) * Math.sin(dLon/2);
  const c = 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1-a));
  return R * c;
}

window.wgRouteCalculateNow = async function() {
  if (routePoints.length < 2) {
    showTemporaryToast('Selecione pelo menos 2 pontos para traçar a rota terrestre.');
    return;
  }

  const calcBtn = document.getElementById('wgRouteCalcBtn');
  const calcBtnText = document.getElementById('wgRouteCalcBtnText');
  const legsContainer = document.getElementById('wgRpLegs');
  const summaryContainer = document.getElementById('wgRpSummary');

  if (calcBtn) calcBtn.disabled = true;
  if (calcBtnText) calcBtnText.textContent = 'TRAÇANDO VIA TERRESTRE...';

  const totalMinNota = parseInt(document.getElementById('wgRpNotaMin')?.value || '60', 10);
  const totalMinDescarga = parseInt(document.getElementById('wgRpDescargaMin')?.value || '90', 10);

  let routeResult = null;

  // 1. Tenta calcular via backend /api/route/terrestrial (OSRM Open Source)
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/route/terrestrial', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ points: routePoints, roundTrip: true })
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.success && Array.isArray(data.coordinates) && data.coordinates.length > 0) {
        routeResult = data;
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Erro ao consultar /api/route/terrestrial:', err);
  }

  // 2. Fallback direto no cliente ao OSRM (Open Source Routing Machine)
  if (!routeResult) {
    try {
      const fullPoints = [...routePoints];
      if (fullPoints.length >= 2) {
        fullPoints.push({ ...fullPoints[0], name: `${fullPoints[0].name || 'Ponto 1'} (Retorno)` });
      }
      const coordStr = fullPoints.map(p => `${p.lng.toFixed(6)},${p.lat.toFixed(6)}`).join(';');
      const osrmDirectRes = await fetch(`https://router.project-osrm.org/route/v1/driving/${coordStr}?overview=full&geometries=geojson&steps=true&annotations=distance,duration`);
      if (osrmDirectRes.ok) {
        const osrmData = await osrmDirectRes.json();
        if (osrmData.code === 'Ok' && osrmData.routes && osrmData.routes.length > 0) {
          const pr = osrmData.routes[0];
          const distKm = Math.round(((pr.distance || 0) / 1000) * 10) / 10;
          const durMin = Math.round((pr.duration || 0) / 60);
          const coords = (pr.geometry?.coordinates || []).map(c => [c[1], c[0]]);
          const legs = (pr.legs || []).map((l, idx) => {
            const fp = fullPoints[idx];
            const tp = fullPoints[idx + 1] || fullPoints[0];
            const roadNames = Array.from(new Set(
              (l.steps || []).map(s => s.name).filter(n => n && n.trim().length > 0)
            )).slice(0, 3).join(', ');
            return {
              stepIndex: idx + 1,
              from: fp.name,
              to: tp.name,
              distanceKm: Math.round(((l.distance || 0) / 1000) * 10) / 10,
              durationMinutes: Math.round((l.duration || 0) / 60),
              summary: roadNames || 'Rodovia Terrestre'
            };
          });

          routeResult = {
            success: true,
            engine: 'OSRM (Open Source Routing Machine)',
            license: 'Open Source / OpenStreetMap Data',
            totalDistanceKm: distKm,
            totalDurationMinutes: durMin,
            coordinates: coords,
            legs: legs
          };
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Fallback direto OSRM falhou:', e);
    }
  }

  // 3. Fallback de contingência matemática caso esteja 100% offline
  if (!routeResult) {
    let fallbackKm = 0;
    const fallbackLegs = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < routePoints.length - 1; i++) {
      const p1 = routePoints[i];
      const p2 = routePoints[i + 1];
      const dist = calculateHaversineKm(p1.lat, p1.lng, p2.lat, p2.lng) * 1.25;
      fallbackKm += dist;
      fallbackLegs.push({
        stepIndex: i + 1,
        from: p1.name,
        to: p2.name,
        distanceKm: Math.round(dist),
        durationMinutes: Math.round((dist / 70) * 60),
        summary: 'Trajeto Rodoviário Estimado'
      });
    }
    const lastP = routePoints[routePoints.length - 1];
    const firstP = routePoints[0];
    const returnDist = calculateHaversineKm(lastP.lat, lastP.lng, firstP.lat, firstP.lng) * 1.25;
    fallbackKm += returnDist;
    fallbackLegs.push({
      stepIndex: fallbackLegs.length + 1,
      from: lastP.name,
      to: `${firstP.name} (Retorno)`,
      distanceKm: Math.round(returnDist),
      durationMinutes: Math.round((returnDist / 70) * 60),
      summary: 'Retorno Rodoviário'
    });

    const fallbackCoords = [...routePoints.map(p => [p.lat, p.lng]), [firstP.lat, firstP.lng]];
    routeResult = {
      success: true,
      engine: 'Estimativa Terrestre',
      license: 'Malha Básica',
      totalDistanceKm: Math.round(fallbackKm),
      totalDurationMinutes: Math.round((fallbackKm / 70) * 60),
      coordinates: fallbackCoords,
      legs: fallbackLegs
    };
  }

  // Cálculos operacionais de tempo
  const totalKm = routeResult.totalDistanceKm;
  const driveMinutes = routeResult.totalDurationMinutes || Math.round((totalKm / 70) * 60);
  const driveHours = driveMinutes / 60;
  const operationalHours = driveHours + (totalMinNota + totalMinDescarga) / 60;

  // Renderiza trechos no painel
  if (legsContainer) {
    legsContainer.innerHTML = `
      <div class="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-slate-500 dark:text-cyan-400 mb-2 flex items-center justify-between">
        <span>Trechos Terrestres (${routeResult.legs.length})</span>
        <span class="text-[9px] font-bold text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400 bg-cyan-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">OSRM</span>
      </div>
      <div class="space-y-2 mb-3">
        ${routeResult.legs.map(l => `
          <div class="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-white/5 border border-slate-200 dark:border-white/10 hover:border-cyan-500/50 transition-colors">
            <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
              <div class="min-w-0">
                <div class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 truncate">${l.from} → ${l.to}</div>
                <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5">
                  <i data-lucide="milestone" class="w-3 h-3 text-cyan-500 shrink-0"></i>
                  <span class="truncate">${l.summary || 'Rodovia Terrestre'}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="text-right shrink-0">
                <span class="text-xs font-black text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400">${l.distanceKm} km</span>
                <div class="text-[9px] text-slate-400">${Math.floor(l.durationMinutes / 60)}h ${l.durationMinutes % 60}m</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `).join('')}
      </div>
    `;
    if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
  }

  // Renderiza resumo no painel
  if (summaryContainer) {
    summaryContainer.innerHTML = `
      <div class="wg-rp-summary space-y-2.5 p-3 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-cyan-950/40 to-slate-900/40 border border-cyan-500/30">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-cyan-500/20 pb-2">
          <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300">Distância Total Terrestre:</span>
          <span class="text-base font-black text-cyan-400">${totalKm} km</span>
        </div>
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
          <span class="text-slate-400">Tempo de Condução (Rodovia):</span>
          <span class="font-bold text-slate-200">${Math.floor(driveHours)}h ${Math.round(driveMinutes % 60)}min</span>
        </div>
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
          <span class="text-slate-400">Tempo Total c/ Paradas:</span>
          <span class="font-black text-emerald-400">${Math.floor(operationalHours)}h ${Math.round((operationalHours % 1) * 60)}min</span>
        </div>
        <div class="pt-1.5 border-t border-cyan-500/20 flex items-center justify-between text-[9px] text-cyan-300/80">
          <span class="flex items-center gap-1">
            <span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-cyan-400 animate-pulse"></span>
            Precisão Terrestre Open Source (OSRM)
          </span>
          <span class="font-mono text-[9px]">OpenStreetMap</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  // Renderiza no mapa a rota terrestre de alta fidelidade
  if (map && routeResult.coordinates && routeResult.coordinates.length > 0) {
    if (routePolyline) map.removeLayer(routePolyline);
    if (routeGlowPolyline) map.removeLayer(routeGlowPolyline);
    routeMarkers.forEach(m => map.removeLayer(m));
    routeMarkers = [];

    // Linha de contorno / brilho inferior para destaque contra satélite/mapa
    routeGlowPolyline = L.polyline(routeResult.coordinates, {
      color: '#0891b2',
      weight: 8,
      opacity: 0.35,
      lineCap: 'round',
      lineJoin: 'round'
    }).addTo(map);

    // Linha principal da rota terrestre precisa
    routePolyline = L.polyline(routeResult.coordinates, {
      color: '#06b6d4',
      weight: 4.5,
      opacity: 0.95,
      lineCap: 'round',
      lineJoin: 'round'
    }).addTo(map);

    routePolyline.bindTooltip(`🛣️ <b>Rota Terrestre (OSRM)</b>: ${totalKm} km • ~${Math.floor(driveHours)}h ${Math.round(driveMinutes % 60)}min de condução`, {
      sticky: true,
      direction: 'top',
      className: 'wg-route-tooltip'
    });

    // Marcadores dos pontos de parada
    routePoints.forEach((p, idx) => {
      const isStart = idx === 0;
      const isEnd = idx === routePoints.length - 1;
      const bgColor = isStart ? '#059669' : isEnd ? '#dc2626' : '#0284c7';
      const label = isStart ? 'Partida' : isEnd ? 'Destino Final' : `Parada ${idx}`;

      const marker = L.marker([p.lat, p.lng], {
        icon: L.divIcon({
          className: 'wg-route-waypoint',
          html: `<div style="background:${bgColor};color:#fff;width:26px;height:26px;border-radius:50%;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-weight:900;font-size:12px;border:2.5px solid #fff;box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.5);">${idx + 1}</div>`,
          iconSize: [26, 26],
          iconAnchor: [13, 13]
        })
      }).addTo(map).bindPopup(`
        <div class="p-2 space-y-1 text-slate-900">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span style="background:${bgColor};" class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full inline-block"></span>
            <span class="font-black text-xs">Ponto ${idx + 1}: ${p.name}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-semibold">${label}</div>
          <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono">${p.lat.toFixed(4)}, ${p.lng.toFixed(4)}</div>
        </div>
      `);
      routeMarkers.push(marker);
    });

    map.fitBounds(routePolyline.getBounds(), { padding: [60, 60], maxZoom: 14 });
  }

  if (calcBtn) calcBtn.disabled = false;
  if (calcBtnText) calcBtnText.textContent = 'TRAÇAR ROTAS';

  showTemporaryToast(`Rota terrestre traçada com sucesso (${totalKm} km via OSRM)!`);
};

window.wgRoutePickOnMap = function() {
  showTemporaryToast('Clique em qualquer local no mapa para adicionar ponto.');
  if (map) {
    const onMapClick = function(e) {
      routePoints.push({
        id: Date.now(),
        name: `Ponto Mapa (${e.latlng.lat.toFixed(2)}, ${e.latlng.lng.toFixed(2)})`,
        lat: e.latlng.lat,
        lng: e.latlng.lng
      });
      renderRoutePoints();
      map.off('click', onMapClick);
      showTemporaryToast('Ponto adicionado à rota!');
    };
    map.once('click', onMapClick);
  }
};

window.wgRouteRecalculate = function() {
  if (routePoints.length >= 2) {
    wgRouteCalculateNow();
  }
};

window.wgRouteSetLastPoint = function(val) {
  showTemporaryToast(`Último ponto definido como Ponto ${parseInt(val, 10) + 1}`);
};

// 9. Modo Edição de Bases
window.openEditBasesModal = function() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('editBasesModal');
  if (!modal) return;
  modal.classList.remove('hidden');
  renderEditBasesTable();
  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
};

window.closeEditBasesModal = function() {
  document.getElementById('editBasesModal')?.classList.add('hidden');
};

function renderEditBasesTable() {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('editBasesTableBody');
  if (!tbody) return;

  if (operationalCenters.length === 0) {
    tbody.innerHTML = `
      <tr>
        <td colspan="6" class="p-6 text-center text-slate-400">
          Nenhuma base cadastrada no sistema. Clique em "Adicionar Nova Base" para cadastrar.
        </td>
      </tr>
    `;
    return;
  }

  tbody.innerHTML = operationalCenters.map(b => `
    <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-white/5 transition">
      <td class="p-2.5 font-mono font-bold text-slate-500">${b.centro || b.centroVirtual || '—'}</td>
      <td class="p-2.5">
        <div class="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">${b.base}</div>
        <div class="text-[10px] font-mono text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400">${b.centroVirtual}</div>
      </td>
      <td class="p-2.5">
        <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-black bg-slate-200 dark:bg-black/40 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">${b.regional}</span>
      </td>
      <td class="p-2.5 font-mono text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">${b.lat}</td>
      <td class="p-2.5 font-mono text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">${b.lng}</td>
      <td class="p-2.5 text-center">
        <button onclick="deleteBaseManual('${b.id}')" class="p-1 text-red-500 hover:text-red-400" title="Excluir base">
          <i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-4 h-4 inline"></i>
        </button>
      </td>
    </tr>
  `).join('');
  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
}

window.addNewBaseManual = function() {
  const nome = window.prompt('Nome da Nova Base:');
  if (!nome) return;
  const codigo = window.prompt('Código / Centro Virtual (Ex: BANVO):') || 'BANVO';
  const reg = window.prompt('Regional (OPC, OPNS, OPN, OPSUL):') || 'OPC';
  const latStr = window.prompt('Latitude (Ex: -15.7988):') || '-15.7988';
  const lngStr = window.prompt('Longitude (Ex: -47.9757):') || '-47.9757';

  const newBase = {
    id: String(Date.now()),
    centro: codigo.toUpperCase().trim(),
    centroVirtual: codigo.toUpperCase().trim(),
    base: nome.trim(),
    regional: reg.toUpperCase().trim(),
    lat: parseFloat(latStr) || -15.7988,
    lng: parseFloat(lngStr) || -47.9757,
    climaExtremo: { ativo: false, tipo: '', descricao: '', severidade: 'NORMAL' }
  };

  operationalCenters.push(newBase);
  localStorage.setItem('basesOperacionais_custom_v1', JSON.stringify(operationalCenters));
  renderCentersList();
  renderMarkers();
  renderEditBasesTable();
  showTemporaryToast('Nova base adicionada com sucesso!');
};

window.deleteBaseManual = function(id) {
  if (confirm('Tem certeza que deseja excluir esta base operacional?')) {
    operationalCenters = operationalCenters.filter(b => b.id !== id);
    localStorage.setItem('basesOperacionais_custom_v1', JSON.stringify(operationalCenters));
    renderCentersList();
    renderMarkers();
    renderEditBasesTable();
    showTemporaryToast('Base excluída!');
  }
};

// 10. INICIALIZAÇÃO COMPLETA DA APLICAÇÃO
function initializeApplication() {
  loadWeatherAlertHistory();
  setupNewsSidebarScrolling();
  disableAlertSound();
  initMap();
  start3MinuteSyncScheduler();
  triggerFullWeatherSync({ isAuto: true });
  renderCentersList();
  renderEmergencyHubList();
  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
}

if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initializeApplication);
} else {
  initializeApplication();
}
  </script>
</body>
</html>

  return {
    plugins: [react(), tailwindcss()],
    resolve: {
      alias: {
        '@': path.resolve(__dirname, '.'),
      },
    },
    server: {
      // HMR is disabled in AI Studio via DISABLE_HMR env var.
      // Do not modifyâfile watching is disabled to prevent flickering during agent edits.
      hmr: process.env.DISABLE_HMR !== 'true',
      // Disable file watching when DISABLE_HMR is true to save CPU during agent edits.
      watch: process.env.DISABLE_HMR === 'true' ? null : {},
    },
  };
});

### ARQUIVO 12/13: public/sw.js
### DESCRIÇÃO: Service Worker PWA Offline
### TOTAL DE LINHAS: 24
################################################################################

self.addEventListener('install', (event) => {
  self.skipWaiting();
});

self.addEventListener('activate', (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(self.clients.claim());
});

self.addEventListener('notificationclick', (event) => {
  event.notification.close();
  event.waitUntil(
    clients.matchAll({ type: 'window', includeUncontrolled: true }).then((clientList) => {
      for (const client of clientList) {
        if (client.url && 'focus' in client) {
          return client.focus();
        }
      }
      if (clients.openWindow) {
        return clients.openWindow('/');
      }
    })
  );
});


### [FIM DO ARQUIVO: public/sw.js] ###


################################################################################
### ARQUIVO 13/13: public/weather-alerts-sw.js
### DESCRIÇÃO: Service Worker Dedicado aos Alertas em Segundo Plano
### TOTAL DE LINHAS: 25
################################################################################

self.addEventListener('install', (event) => {
  self.skipWaiting();
});

self.addEventListener('activate', (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(self.clients.claim());
});

self.addEventListener('notificationclick', (event) => {
  event.notification.close();
  const targetUrl = event.notification.data?.url || '/';
  event.waitUntil(
    clients.matchAll({ type: 'window', includeUncontrolled: true }).then((clientList) => {
      for (const client of clientList) {
        if ('focus' in client) {
          return client.focus();
        }
      }
      if (clients.openWindow) {
        return clients.openWindow(targetUrl);
      }
    })
  );
});
